1	import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.224.0/http/server.ts";
2	import { NO_SESSION_RUNTIME_OPTIONS, withEdgeRequest } from "../_shared/edgeHandler.ts";
3	import { toErrorMessage } from "../_shared/errorMessage.ts";
4	import { jsonResponse } from "../_shared/http.ts";
5	import { persistSessionState } from "./db.ts";
6	import { isRecord, normalizeSessionRow } from "./llm/messages.ts";
7	import { runAgentLoop } from "./loop.ts";
8	import { enrichClipsWithPrompts, normalizeSelectedClips } from "./selectedClips.ts";
9	import { TIMELINE_AGENT_TOOLS } from "./tool-schemas.ts";
10	import type { AgentInvocationBody } from "./types.ts";
11	
12	serve((req) => withEdgeRequest<AgentInvocationBody>(
13	  req,
14	  {
15	    functionName: "ai-timeline-agent",
16	    logPrefix: "[AI-TIMELINE-AGENT]",
17	    method: "POST",
18	    parseBody: "strict",
19	    auth: { required: true, options: { allowJwtUserAuth: true } },
20	    ...NO_SESSION_RUNTIME_OPTIONS,
21	  },
22	  async ({ auth, body, logger, supabaseAdmin }) => {
23	    if (!auth?.userId) return jsonResponse({ error: "Authentication failed" }, 401);
24	    const sessionId = typeof body.session_id === "string" ? body.session_id.trim() : "";
25	    const userMessage = body.user_message === undefined || body.user_message === null
26	      ? undefined
27	      : typeof body.user_message === "string" ? body.user_message.trim() : null;
28	    const selectedClips = normalizeSelectedClips(body.selected_clips);
29	    if (!sessionId) return jsonResponse({ error: "session_id is required" }, 400);
30	    if (userMessage === null) return jsonResponse({ error: "user_message must be a string when provided" }, 400);
31	
32	    const { data: rawSession, error: sessionError } = await supabaseAdmin.from("timeline_agent_sessions").select("*").eq("id", sessionId).maybeSingle();
33	    if (sessionError) {
34	      logger.error("Failed to load session", { session_id: sessionId, error: sessionError.message });
35	      return jsonResponse({ error: "Failed to load session" }, 500);
36	    }
37	    if (!rawSession || !isRecord(rawSession)) return jsonResponse({ error: "Session not found" }, 404);
38	
39	    const session = normalizeSessionRow(rawSession);
40	    if (session.user_id !== auth.userId) return jsonResponse({ error: "Forbidden" }, 403);
41	    if (session.status === "cancelled") {
42	      logger.warn("Rejected invoke for cancelled session", {
43	        session_id: session.id,
44	        user_id: auth.userId,
45	        status: session.status,
46	        cancelled_at: session.cancelled_at,
47	        cancelled_by: session.cancelled_by,
48	        cancel_source: session.cancel_source,
49	        cancel_reason: session.cancel_reason,
50	      });
51	      return jsonResponse({
52	        error: "Session cancelled",
53	        status: "cancelled",
54	        cancelled_at: session.cancelled_at,
55	        cancelled_by: session.cancelled_by,
56	        cancel_source: session.cancel_source,
57	        cancel_reason: session.cancel_reason,
58	      }, 409);
59	    }
60	
61	    const lastTurn = session.turns[session.turns.length - 1];
62	    const nextUserMessage = userMessage && lastTurn?.role === "user" && lastTurn.content === userMessage ? undefined : userMessage;
63	
64	    try {
65	      const enrichedSelectedClips = await enrichClipsWithPrompts(supabaseAdmin, selectedClips);
66	      const result = await runAgentLoop({
67	        session,
68	        userMessage: nextUserMessage,
69	        selectedClips: enrichedSelectedClips,
70	        supabaseAdmin,
71	        logger,
72	      });
73	      await persistSessionState(supabaseAdmin, {
74	        sessionId: session.id,
75	        status: result.status,
76	        turns: result.turns,
77	        summary: result.summary,
78	      });
79	      return jsonResponse({
80	        session_id: session.id,
81	        status: result.status,
82	        turns_added: result.turns.length - session.turns.length,
83	        tool_count: TIMELINE_AGENT_TOOLS.length,
84	        model: session.model,
85	      });
86	    } catch (error: unknown) {
87	      return jsonResponse({
88	        error: "Failed to process timeline agent request",
89	        details: toErrorMessage(error),
90	      }, 500);
91	    }
92	  },
93	));
94

65	function summarizeToolCallArgs(toolName: string, args: Record<string, unknown> | undefined): string {
66	  if (!args) return "";
67	  if (toolName === "create_task") {
68	    // Keep the fields that matter for continuity
69	    const parts: string[] = [];
70	    if (args.task_type) parts.push(`task_type=${args.task_type}`);
71	    if (args.prompt) parts.push(`prompt="${String(args.prompt).slice(0, 150)}"`);
72	    if (args.count) parts.push(`count=${args.count}`);
73	    if (args.model) parts.push(`model=${args.model}`);
74	    if (Array.isArray(args.reference_image_urls) && args.reference_image_urls.length > 0) {
75	      parts.push(`reference_image_urls=${JSON.stringify(args.reference_image_urls)}`);
76	    }
77	    return parts.join(", ");
78	  }
79	  return JSON.stringify(args).slice(0, 200);
80	}
81	
82	function turnsToTranscript(turns: AgentTurn[]): string {
83	  const lines: string[] = [];
84	  for (const turn of turns) {
85	    if (turn.role === "user") {
86	      lines.push(`USER: ${turn.content}`);
87	    } else if (turn.role === "assistant") {
88	      lines.push(`ASSISTANT: ${turn.content}`);
89	    } else if (turn.role === "tool_call") {
90	      lines.push(`TOOL CALL ${turn.tool_name}: ${summarizeToolCallArgs(turn.tool_name ?? "", turn.tool_args)}`);
91	    } else if (turn.role === "tool_result") {
92	      lines.push(`TOOL RESULT ${turn.tool_name}: ${turn.content}`);
93	    }
94	  }
95	  return lines.join("\n");
96	}
97	
98	async function summarizeTurns(
99	  turnsToSummarize: AgentTurn[],
100	  existingSummary: string | null,
101	  logger: LoopLogger,
102	): Promise<string | null> {
103	  const apiKey = Deno.env.get("GROQ_API_KEY");
104	  if (!apiKey) return existingSummary;
105	
106	  const transcript = turnsToTranscript(turnsToSummarize);
107	  if (!transcript.trim()) return existingSummary;
108	
109	  const systemMsg = `You summarize AI agent conversation history for an image/video generation tool. Write a concise summary that captures:
110	
111	1. What the user originally asked for and their creative direction
112	2. What tasks were created (task types, counts, key prompts used)
113	3. What reference images were used (include URLs so they can be reused)
114	4. The user's latest feedback or direction — what they liked, what they want changed
115	5. Any unresolved requests or issues
116	
117	Keep it factual and compact. Use bullet points. Include specific URLs and prompt text — these are needed for follow-up requests.`;
118	
119	  const userMsg = existingSummary
120	    ? `Here is the existing summary of earlier conversation:\n${existingSummary}\n\nHere is the new conversation to incorporate:\n${transcript}\n\nProduce an updated combined summary.`
121	    : `Summarize this conversation:\n${transcript}`;
122	
123	  try {
124	    const groq = new Groq({ apiKey, timeout: GROQ_TIMEOUT_MS });
125	    const resp = await groq.chat.completions.create({
126	      model: GROQ_TRIAGE_MODEL,
127	      messages: [
128	        { role: "system", content: systemMsg },
129	        { role: "user", content: userMsg },
130	      ],
131	      temperature: 0.1,
132	      max_tokens: 1024,
133	    });
134	    const summary = resp.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim();
135	    if (summary) {
136	      logger.info("Session summarized", { oldTurns: turnsToSummarize.length, summaryLength: summary.length });
137	      return summary;
138	    }
139	  } catch (err: unknown) {
140	    logger.error("Failed to summarize session", { error: toErrorMessage(err) });
141	  }
142	  return existingSummary;
143	}
144	
145	const MAX_TURNS_TO_SUMMARIZE = 30; // Cap per summarization call to avoid token limits
146	
147	async function maybeCompressSession(
148	  turns: AgentTurn[],
149	  existingSummary: string | null,
150	  logger: LoopLogger,
151	): Promise<{ turns: AgentTurn[]; summary: string | null }> {
152	  if (turns.length <= SUMMARIZE_THRESHOLD) {
153	    return { turns, summary: existingSummary };
154	  }
155	
156	  // Keep the most recent turns, summarize a bounded chunk of older ones
157	  const keepCount = Math.floor(SUMMARIZE_THRESHOLD / 2);
158	  const olderTurns = turns.slice(0, turns.length - keepCount);
159	  const recentTurns = turns.slice(turns.length - keepCount);
160	
161	  // Only summarize the most recent chunk of older turns (closest to current context)
162	  // If there's a huge backlog, we drop the oldest turns — the existing summary
163	  // (if any) already covers earlier history
164	  const turnsToSummarize = olderTurns.length > MAX_TURNS_TO_SUMMARIZE
165	    ? olderTurns.slice(-MAX_TURNS_TO_SUMMARIZE)
166	    : olderTurns;
167	
168	  const summary = await summarizeTurns(turnsToSummarize, existingSummary, logger);
169	  return { turns: recentTurns, summary };
170	}

186	function attachSelectedClips(
187	  turn: AgentTurn,
188	  selectedClips?: SelectedClipPayload[],
189	) {
190	  if (!selectedClips?.length) {
191	    return;
192	  }
193	
194	  turn.attachments = selectedClips.map((clip) => ({
195	    clipId: clip.clip_id,
196	    url: clip.url,
197	    mediaType: clip.media_type,
198	    ...(typeof clip.is_timeline_backed === "boolean"
199	      ? { isTimelineBacked: clip.is_timeline_backed }
200	      : {}),
201	    ...(clip.generation_id ? { generationId: clip.generation_id } : {}),
202	    ...(clip.prompt ? { prompt: clip.prompt } : {}),
203	    ...(clip.shot_id ? { shotId: clip.shot_id } : {}),
204	    ...(clip.shot_name ? { shotName: clip.shot_name } : {}),
205	    ...(typeof clip.shot_selection_clip_count === "number"
206	      ? { shotSelectionClipCount: clip.shot_selection_clip_count }
207	      : {}),
208	    ...(clip.track_id ? { trackId: clip.track_id } : {}),
209	    ...(typeof clip.at === "number" ? { at: clip.at } : {}),
210	    ...(typeof clip.duration === "number" ? { duration: clip.duration } : {}),
211	    ...(clip.timeline_placement ? { timelinePlacement: clip.timeline_placement } : {}),
212	  }));
213	}
214	
215	function enrichSelectedClipsWithTimelinePlacement(
216	  selectedClips: SelectedClipPayload[] | undefined,
217	  timelineState: TimelineState,
218	  timelineId: string,
219	): SelectedClipPayload[] | undefined {
220	  if (!selectedClips?.length) {
221	    return selectedClips;
222	  }
223	
224	  const timelineBackedClips = selectedClips.filter((clip) => clip.is_timeline_backed);
225	  if (timelineBackedClips.length !== 1) {
226	    return selectedClips;
227	  }
228	
229	  const sourceClip = timelineBackedClips[0];
230	  const timelinePlacement = resolveTimelinePlacement(sourceClip, timelineState, timelineId);
231	  if (!timelinePlacement) {
232	    return selectedClips;
233	  }
234	
235	  return selectedClips.map((clip) => (
236	    clip.clip_id === sourceClip.clip_id
237	      ? { ...clip, timeline_placement: timelinePlacement }
238	      : clip
239	  ));
240	}
241	
242	export function recoverSelectedClipsFromTurns(turns: AgentTurn[]): SelectedClipPayload[] {
243	  for (let index = turns.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
244	    const turn = turns[index];
245	    if (turn?.role !== "user" || !Array.isArray(turn.attachments) || turn.attachments.length === 0) {
246	      continue;
247	    }
248	
249	    return turn.attachments.flatMap((attachment) => {
250	      if (!isRecord(attachment)) {
251	        return [];
252	      }
253	
254	      const clipId = typeof attachment.clipId === "string" ? attachment.clipId.trim() : "";
255	      const url = typeof attachment.url === "string" ? attachment.url.trim() : "";
256	      const mediaType = attachment.mediaType;
257	      const generationId = typeof attachment.generationId === "string" && attachment.generationId.trim()
258	        ? attachment.generationId.trim()
259	        : undefined;
260	      const prompt = typeof attachment.prompt === "string" && attachment.prompt.trim()
261	        ? attachment.prompt.trim()
262	        : undefined;
263	      const isTimelineBacked = typeof attachment.isTimelineBacked === "boolean"
264	        ? attachment.isTimelineBacked
265	        : undefined;
266	      const shotId = typeof attachment.shotId === "string" && attachment.shotId.trim()
267	        ? attachment.shotId.trim()
268	        : undefined;
269	      const shotName = typeof attachment.shotName === "string" && attachment.shotName.trim()
270	        ? attachment.shotName.trim()
271	        : undefined;
272	      const trackId = typeof attachment.trackId === "string" && attachment.trackId.trim()
273	        ? attachment.trackId.trim()
274	        : undefined;
275	      const at = typeof attachment.at === "number" && Number.isFinite(attachment.at)
276	        ? attachment.at
277	        : undefined;
278	      const duration = typeof attachment.duration === "number"
279	        && Number.isFinite(attachment.duration)
280	        && attachment.duration >= 0
281	        ? attachment.duration
282	        : undefined;
283	      const timelinePlacement = isRecord(attachment.timelinePlacement)
284	        && typeof attachment.timelinePlacement.timeline_id === "string"
285	        && attachment.timelinePlacement.timeline_id.trim()
286	        && typeof attachment.timelinePlacement.source_clip_id === "string"
287	        && attachment.timelinePlacement.source_clip_id.trim()
288	        && typeof attachment.timelinePlacement.target_track === "string"
289	        && attachment.timelinePlacement.target_track.trim()
290	        && typeof attachment.timelinePlacement.insertion_time === "number"
291	        && Number.isFinite(attachment.timelinePlacement.insertion_time)
292	        && (attachment.timelinePlacement.intent === "after_source" || attachment.timelinePlacement.intent === "replace")
293	        ? {
294	          timeline_id: attachment.timelinePlacement.timeline_id.trim(),
295	          source_clip_id: attachment.timelinePlacement.source_clip_id.trim(),
296	          target_track: attachment.timelinePlacement.target_track.trim(),
297	          insertion_time: attachment.timelinePlacement.insertion_time,
298	          intent: attachment.timelinePlacement.intent,
299	        }
300	        : undefined;
301	      const shotSelectionClipCount = typeof attachment.shotSelectionClipCount === "number"
302	        && Number.isFinite(attachment.shotSelectionClipCount)
303	        && attachment.shotSelectionClipCount > 0
304	        ? attachment.shotSelectionClipCount
305	        : undefined;
306	
307	      if (!clipId || !url || (mediaType !== "image" && mediaType !== "video")) {
308	        return [];
309	      }
310	
311	      return [{
312	        clip_id: clipId,
313	        url,
314	        media_type: mediaType,
315	        ...(generationId ? { generation_id: generationId } : {}),
316	        ...(prompt ? { prompt } : {}),

323	        ...(timelinePlacement ? { timeline_placement: timelinePlacement } : {}),

349	export function cleanAssistantText(text: string): string {
350	  return text
351	    .replace(/\[TOOL_CALL\][\s\S]*?\[\/TOOL_CALL\]/g, "")
352	    .replace(/<invoke[\s\S]*?<\/invoke>/g, "")
353	    .replace(/Tool call \w+:[\s\S]*?(?=\n\n|$)/g, "")
354	    .replace(/(?:run|create_task)\s*\([^)]*\)/g, "")
355	    .replace(/^\s*\d+[.):-]\s*(set-text|move|trim|delete|duplicate|add-text|set|view|find-issues|generate)\b.*$/gm, "")
356	    .trim();
357	}

359	async function executeCreateShot(
360	  args: Record<string, unknown>,
361	  timelineState: TimelineState,
362	  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseAdmin,
363	): Promise<Pick<ToolResult, "result">> {
364	  const shotName = asTrimmedString(args.shot_name);
365	  const generationIds = asStringArray(args.generation_ids);
366	
367	  if (!shotName) {
368	    return { result: "create_shot requires shot_name." };
369	  }
370	
371	  if (generationIds.length === 0) {
372	    return { result: "create_shot requires at least one generation_id." };
373	  }
374	
375	  const shotId = await createShotWithGenerations(supabaseAdmin, {
376	    projectId: timelineState.projectId,
377	    shotName,
378	    generationIds,
379	    position: APPEND_SHOT_POSITION,
380	  });
381	
382	  return {
383	    result: `Created shot ${shotName} (${shotId}) with ${generationIds.length} generation(s).`,
384	  };
385	}
386	
387	async function executeGetTasks(
388	  args: Record<string, unknown>,
389	  timelineState: TimelineState,
390	  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseAdmin,
391	): Promise<ToolResult> {
392	  const tasks = await fetchProjectTasks(supabaseAdmin, timelineState.projectId, {
393	    status: typeof args.status === "string" ? args.status : undefined,
394	    taskId: typeof args.task_id === "string" ? args.task_id : undefined,
395	    limit: typeof args.limit === "number" ? args.limit : undefined,
396	  });
397	
398	  if (tasks.length === 0) {
399	    return { result: "No tasks found." };
400	  }
401	
402	  const lines = tasks.map((t) => {
403	    const age = timeSince(t.created_at);
404	    let line = `• ${t.id.slice(0, 8)} | ${t.task_type} | ${t.status} | ${age}`;
405	    if (t.params_summary) line += ` | ${t.params_summary}`;
406	    if (t.error_message) line += `\n  Error: ${t.error_message.slice(0, 200)}`;
407	    if (t.status === "In Progress" && t.generation_started_at) {
408	      line += ` | running ${timeSince(t.generation_started_at)}`;
409	    }
410	    if (t.status === "Complete" && t.attempts > 1) {
411	      line += ` | ${t.attempts} attempts`;
412	    }
413	    return line;
414	  });
415	
416	  return { result: `${tasks.length} task(s):\n${lines.join("\n")}` };
417	}
418	
419	function timeSince(iso: string): string {
420	  const ms = Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime();
421	  if (ms < 60_000) return `${Math.round(ms / 1000)}s ago`;
422	  if (ms < 3_600_000) return `${Math.round(ms / 60_000)}m ago`;
423	  return `${Math.round(ms / 3_600_000)}h ago`;
424	}
425	
426	export async function executeToolCall(
427	  toolCall: ExtractedToolCall,
428	  timelineState: TimelineState,
429	  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseAdmin,
430	  timelineId: string,
431	  selectedClips?: SelectedClipPayload[],
432	  generationContext?: GenerationContext,
433	  userId?: string,
434	): Promise<ToolResult> {
435	  const toolArgs = toolCall.args;
436	
437	  if (toolCall.parseError) {
438	    return { result: toolCall.parseError };
439	  }
440	
441	  if (toolCall.name === "run") {
442	    return typeof toolArgs.command === "string" && toolArgs.command.trim()
443	      ? await executeCommand(
444	        toolArgs.command,
445	        timelineState,
446	        timelineId,
447	        supabaseAdmin,
448	      )
449	      : { result: "run requires command." };
450	  }
451	
452	  if (toolCall.name === "create_task") {
453	    return await executeCreateTask(toolArgs, timelineState, selectedClips, supabaseAdmin, generationContext, timelineId);
454	  }
455	
456	  if (toolCall.name === "transform_image") {
457	    return await executeTransformImage(toolArgs, selectedClips, userId);
458	  }
459	
460	  if (toolCall.name === "search_loras") {
461	    return await executeSearchLoras(toolArgs, supabaseAdmin, userId);
462	  }
463	
464	  if (toolCall.name === "set_lora") {
465	    return await executeSetLora(toolArgs, timelineState, selectedClips, supabaseAdmin, generationContext);
466	  }
467	
468	  if (toolCall.name === "duplicate_generation") {
469	    return await executeDuplicateGeneration(toolArgs, timelineState, selectedClips, supabaseAdmin);
470	  }
471	
472	  if (toolCall.name === "create_shot") {
473	    return await executeCreateShot(toolArgs, timelineState, supabaseAdmin);
474	  }
475	
476	  if (toolCall.name === "get_tasks") {
477	    return await executeGetTasks(toolArgs, timelineState, supabaseAdmin);
478	  }
479	
480	  return { result: `Unknown tool: ${toolCall.name}.` };
481	}
482	
483	export function buildToolErrorTurn(toolCallId: string, error: unknown): AgentTurn {
484	  return createTurn("assistant", `[TOOL ERROR ${toolCallId}] ${toErrorMessage(error)}`);
485	}
486	
487	async function processToolCalls({
488	  toolCalls,
489	  assistantText,
490	  turns,
491	  messages,
492	  timelineState,
493	  selectedClips,
494	  generationContext,
495	  supabaseAdmin,
496	  timelineId,
497	  userId,
498	  setActiveToolCallId,
499	}: {
500	  toolCalls: ExtractedToolCall[];
501	  assistantText: string;
502	  turns: AgentTurn[];
503	  messages: LlmMessage[];
504	  timelineState: TimelineState;
505	  selectedClips?: SelectedClipPayload[];
506	  generationContext?: GenerationContext;
507	  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseAdmin;
508	  timelineId: string;
509	  userId?: string;
510	  setActiveToolCallId: (toolCallId: string | null) => void;
511	}): Promise<{ hasError: boolean }> {
512	  let hasError = false;
513	
514	  for (const toolCall of toolCalls) {
515	    const toolArgs = toolCall.args;
516	    const toolContent = toolCall.name === "run"
517	      ? (typeof toolArgs.command === "string" ? toolArgs.command : JSON.stringify(toolArgs))
518	      : JSON.stringify(toolArgs);
519	
520	    setActiveToolCallId(toolCall.id);
521	    turns.push(createToolTurn("tool_call", toolCall.name, toolContent, toolArgs));
522	    messages.push({
523	      role: "assistant",
524	      content: null,
525	      tool_calls: [{
526	        id: toolCall.id,
527	        type: "function" as const,
528	        function: { name: toolCall.name, arguments: JSON.stringify(toolArgs) },
529	      }],
530	    });
531	
532	    const result = await executeToolCall(
533	      toolCall,
534	      timelineState,
535	      supabaseAdmin,
536	      timelineId,
537	      selectedClips,
538	      generationContext,
539	      userId,
540	    );
541	    setActiveToolCallId(null);
542	
543	    turns.push(createToolTurn("tool_result", toolCall.name, result.result, toolArgs));
544	    messages.push({
545	      role: "tool",
546	      tool_call_id: toolCall.id,
547	      content: result.result,
548	    });
549	
550	    if (isToolError(result.result)) {
551	      hasError = true;
552	      break;
553	    }
554	  }
555	
556	  setActiveToolCallId(null);
557	
558	  if (assistantText) {
559	    const cleaned = cleanAssistantText(assistantText);
560	    if (cleaned && cleaned.length > 2) {
561	      turns.push(createTurn("assistant", cleaned));
562	    }
563	  }
564	
565	  if (hasError) {
566	    const lastResult = turns[turns.length - 1];
567	    messages.push({
568	      role: "user",
569	      content: `Tool failed: "${lastResult?.content ?? "unknown error"}". You can: (1) run(command="...") with a corrected timeline command, (2) create_task({...}) with corrected task arguments, (3) run(command="view") to inspect the timeline, or (4) reply in plain text to tell the user what went wrong.`,
570	    });
571	  }
572	
573	  return { hasError };
574	}
575	
576	export async function runAgentLoop(
577	  options: RunAgentLoopOptions,
578	): Promise<RunAgentLoopResult> {
579	  const { session, userMessage, selectedClips, supabaseAdmin, logger } = options;
580	  const effectiveSelectedClips = selectedClips?.length
581	    ? selectedClips
582	    : recoverSelectedClipsFromTurns(session.turns);
583	
584	  const lastTurn = session.turns[session.turns.length - 1];
585	  const isDuplicate = userMessage && lastTurn?.role === "user" && lastTurn.content === userMessage;
586	  const turns = [...session.turns];
587	  if (userMessage && !isDuplicate) {
588	    const userTurn = createTurn("user", userMessage);
589	    attachSelectedClips(userTurn, effectiveSelectedClips);
590	    turns.push(userTurn);
591	  }
592	  let status: AgentSessionStatus = "processing";
593	  let activeToolCallId: string | null = null;
594	
595	  let summary = session.summary;
596	
597	  try {
598	    await persistSessionState(supabaseAdmin, {
599	      sessionId: session.id,
600	      status,
601	      turns,
602	      summary,
603	    });
604	
605	    const timelineState = await loadTimelineState(supabaseAdmin, session.timeline_id);
606	    const resolvedSelectedClips = enrichSelectedClipsWithTimelinePlacement(
607	      effectiveSelectedClips,
608	      timelineState,
609	      session.timeline_id,
610	    );
611	    if (userMessage && !isDuplicate) {
612	      const currentUserTurn = turns[turns.length - 1];
613	      if (currentUserTurn?.role === "user") {
614	        attachSelectedClips(currentUserTurn, resolvedSelectedClips);
615	      }
616	    }
617	    const imageSettings = await loadProjectImageSettings(supabaseAdmin, timelineState.projectId);
618	    const clipShotResolution = await resolveSelectedClipShot(
619	      supabaseAdmin,
620	      timelineState,
621	      resolvedSelectedClips,
622	    );
623	    const clipShotId = clipShotResolution.shotId;
624	    const clipShotName = resolveSharedShotName(resolvedSelectedClips, clipShotId, timelineState);
625	    const travelSettings = clipShotId
626	      ? await loadShotVideoTravelSettings(supabaseAdmin, clipShotId)
627	      : null;
628	    const activeReference = imageSettings
629	      ? await loadActiveReference(supabaseAdmin, imageSettings, clipShotId ?? undefined)
630	      : null;
631	    const generationContext: GenerationContext = {
632	      image: imageSettings
633	        ? {
634	          defaultModelName: imageSettings.selectedTextModel ?? "qwen-image",
635	          activeReference,
636	          selectedLorasByCategory: imageSettings.selectedLorasByCategory,
637	        }
638	        : null,
639	      travel: travelSettings,
640	    };
641	    const timelineSummary = viewTimeline(
642	      timelineState.config,
643	      timelineState.registry,
644	      timelineState.shotNamesById,
645	    ).result;
646	    const systemPrompt = buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt({
647	      projectId: timelineState.projectId,
648	      timelineSummary,
649	      selectedClips: resolvedSelectedClips,
650	      defaultModel: generationContext.image?.defaultModelName,
651	      activeReference: generationContext.image?.activeReference ?? null,
652	      travelSettings,
653	      imageLorasByCategory: generationContext.image?.selectedLorasByCategory ?? null,
654	      sharedShotId: clipShotId,
655	      sharedShotName: clipShotName,
656	    });
657	    // Compress long sessions: summarize old turns, keep recent ones
658	    const compressed = await maybeCompressSession(turns, summary, logger);
659	    summary = compressed.summary;
660	    if (compressed.turns.length < turns.length) {
661	      // Replace turns with compressed version
662	      turns.length = 0;
663	      turns.push(...compressed.turns);
664	    }
665	
666	    const messages = buildInitialMessages(systemPrompt, turns, summary);
667	    const startedAt = Date.now();
668	
669	    // Triage the user message to pick provider: easy/okay → Kimi K2.5, hard → Claude
670	    const difficulty = userMessage
671	      ? await triageDifficulty(userMessage, logger)
672	      : "okay";
673	
674	    for (let iteration = 0; iteration < LOOP_LIMIT; iteration += 1) {
675	      if (Date.now() - startedAt >= SOFT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
676	        status = "continue";
677	        break;
678	      }
679	
680	      const currentStatus = await loadSessionStatus(supabaseAdmin, session.id);
681	      if (currentStatus === "cancelled") {
682	        logger.info("Detected cancelled session during agent loop", {
683	          session_id: session.id,
684	          status: currentStatus,
685	        });
686	        status = "cancelled";
687	        break;
688	      }
689	
690	      let completion;
691	      try {
692	        completion = await invokeLlm(
693	          {
694	            messages: messages as unknown[],
695	            tools: TIMELINE_AGENT_TOOLS as unknown[],
696	            tool_choice: "auto",
697	            temperature: 0.2,
698	            max_tokens: 4096,
699	            top_p: 1,
700	          },
701	          difficulty,
702	          logger,
703	        );
704	      } catch (llmError: unknown) {
705	        const text = `AI service error: ${toErrorMessage(llmError)}`;
706	        turns.push(createTurn("assistant", text));
707	        messages.push({ role: "assistant", content: text });
708	        status = "waiting_user";
709	        break;
710	      }
711	
712	      const responseMessage = completion.choices[0]?.message;
713	      if (!responseMessage) {
714	        turns.push(createTurn("assistant", "No response from AI."));
715	        status = "waiting_user";
716	        break;
717	      }
718	
719	      const extractedToolCalls = extractToolCalls(responseMessage);
720	      const assistantText = extractAssistantText(responseMessage);
721	
722	      if (extractedToolCalls.length === 0) {
723	        const text = assistantText || "What would you like me to do?";
724	        turns.push(createTurn("assistant", text));
725	        messages.push({ role: "assistant", content: text });
726	        status = "waiting_user";
727	        break;
728	      }
729	
730	      const { hasError } = await processToolCalls({
731	        toolCalls: extractedToolCalls,
732	        assistantText,
733	        turns,
734	        messages,
735	        timelineState,
736	        selectedClips: resolvedSelectedClips,
737	        generationContext,
738	        supabaseAdmin,
739	        timelineId: session.timeline_id,
740	        userId: session.user_id,
741	        setActiveToolCallId: (toolCallId) => { activeToolCallId = toolCallId; },
742	      });
743	
744	      if (hasError) {
745	        continue;
746	      }
747	    }
748	
749	    if (status === "processing") {
750	      status = "continue";
751	    }
752	
753	    await persistSessionState(supabaseAdmin, {
754	      sessionId: session.id,
755	      status,
756	      turns,
757	      summary,
758	    });
759	
760	    logger.info("Agent loop completed", {
761	      session_id: session.id,
762	      status,
763	      turns_added: turns.length - session.turns.length,
764	    });
765	
766	    return { status, turns, summary };
767	  } catch (error: unknown) {
768	    const errorDetail = toErrorMessage(error);
769	    console.error(`[agent] FATAL: ${errorDetail}`);
770	    logger.error("Agent loop failed", {
771	      session_id: session.id,
772	      error: errorDetail,
773	    });
774	
775	    turns.push(
776	      activeToolCallId
777	        ? buildToolErrorTurn(activeToolCallId, error)
778	        : createTurn("assistant", `[INTERNAL ERROR] ${errorDetail}`),
779	    );
780	
781	    try {
782	      await persistSessionState(supabaseAdmin, {
783	        sessionId: session.id,
784	        status: "error",
785	        turns,
786	        summary,
787	      });
788	    } catch (persistError: unknown) {
789	      logger.error("Failed to persist error state", {
790	        session_id: session.id,
791	        error: toErrorMessage(persistError),
792	      });
793	    }
794	
795	    return { status: "error", turns, summary };
796	  }
797	}
798

1	import {
2	  extractAssistantText,
3	  isRecord,
4	  parseToolArgsSafely,
5	} from "./llm/messages.ts";
6	
7	export type ExtractedToolCall = {
8	  id: string;
9	  name: string;
10	  args: Record<string, unknown>;
11	  parseError: string | null;
12	};
13	
14	const COMMAND_VERBS = /^(?:view|move|split|trim|delete|rm|set|set-text|settext|add-text|addtext|text|swap|duplicate|dup|clone|query|undo|find-issues|findissues|issues|generate|gen)\b/;
15	const FALLBACK_TOOL_NAMES = new Set([
16	  "run",
17	  "create_task",
18	  "duplicate_generation",
19	  "search_loras",
20	  "set_lora",
21	  "create_shot",
22	  "get_tasks",
23	]);
24	
25	function cleanCommand(raw: string): string {
26	  return raw
27	    .replace(/\\"/g, "\"")
28	    .replace(/\\'/g, "'")
29	    .replace(/^["']+|["']+$/g, "")
30	    .trim();
31	}
32	
33	function extractBalancedBlock(
34	  text: string,
35	  startIndex: number,
36	  openChar: string,
37	  closeChar: string,
38	): { content: string; endIndex: number } | null {
39	  if (text[startIndex] !== openChar) {
40	    return null;
41	  }
42	
43	  let depth = 0;
44	  let inString = false;
45	  let stringQuote = "";
46	  let escaped = false;
47	
48	  for (let index = startIndex; index < text.length; index += 1) {
49	    const char = text[index];
50	
51	    if (inString) {
52	      if (escaped) {
53	        escaped = false;
54	        continue;
55	      }
56	      if (char === "\\") {
57	        escaped = true;
58	        continue;
59	      }
60	      if (char === stringQuote) {
61	        inString = false;
62	        stringQuote = "";
63	      }
64	      continue;
65	    }
66	
67	    if (char === "\"" || char === "'") {
68	      inString = true;
69	      stringQuote = char;
70	      continue;
71	    }
72	
73	    if (char === openChar) {
74	      depth += 1;
75	      continue;
76	    }
77	
78	    if (char !== closeChar) {
79	      continue;
80	    }
81	
82	    depth -= 1;
83	    if (depth === 0) {
84	      return {
85	        content: text.slice(startIndex, index + 1),
86	        endIndex: index + 1,
87	      };
88	    }
89	  }
90	
91	  return null;
92	}
93	
94	function buildToolCall(name: string, rawArguments: string): ExtractedToolCall {
95	  const { args, error } = parseToolArgsSafely(rawArguments);
96	  return {
97	    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
98	    name,
99	    args,
100	    parseError: error,
101	  };
102	}
103	
104	function extractTextFormattedToolCalls(text: string): ExtractedToolCall[] {
105	  const toolCalls: ExtractedToolCall[] = [];
106	
107	  const toolCallHeaderRe = /Tool call\s+([a-z_]+)\s*:/gi;
108	  let headerMatch: RegExpExecArray | null;
109	  while ((headerMatch = toolCallHeaderRe.exec(text)) !== null) {
110	    const name = headerMatch[1];
111	    if (!FALLBACK_TOOL_NAMES.has(name)) {
112	      continue;
113	    }
114	
115	    const jsonStart = text.indexOf("{", headerMatch.index + headerMatch[0].length);
116	    if (jsonStart < 0) {
117	      continue;
118	    }
119	
120	    const balancedJson = extractBalancedBlock(text, jsonStart, "{", "}");
121	    if (!balancedJson) {
122	      continue;
123	    }
124	
125	    toolCalls.push(buildToolCall(name, balancedJson.content));
126	    toolCallHeaderRe.lastIndex = balancedJson.endIndex;
127	  }
128	  if (toolCalls.length > 0) {
129	    return toolCalls;
130	  }
131	
132	  const invocationRe = /\b([a-z_]+)\s*\(\s*/gi;
133	  let invocationMatch: RegExpExecArray | null;
134	  while ((invocationMatch = invocationRe.exec(text)) !== null) {
135	    const name = invocationMatch[1];
136	    if (!FALLBACK_TOOL_NAMES.has(name)) {
137	      continue;
138	    }
139	
140	    const argsStart = invocationRe.lastIndex;
141	    const firstNonSpaceIndex = text.slice(argsStart).search(/\S/);
142	    if (firstNonSpaceIndex < 0) {
143	      continue;
144	    }
145	
146	    const jsonStart = argsStart + firstNonSpaceIndex;
147	    if (text[jsonStart] !== "{") {
148	      continue;
149	    }
150	
151	    const balancedJson = extractBalancedBlock(text, jsonStart, "{", "}");
152	    if (!balancedJson) {
153	      continue;
154	    }
155	
156	    const closingParenIndex = text.slice(balancedJson.endIndex).search(/\)/);
157	    if (closingParenIndex < 0) {
158	      continue;
159	    }
160	
161	    toolCalls.push(buildToolCall(name, balancedJson.content));
162	    invocationRe.lastIndex = balancedJson.endIndex + closingParenIndex + 1;
163	  }
164	
165	  return toolCalls;
166	}
167	
168	export function extractToolCalls(responseMessage: Record<string, unknown>): ExtractedToolCall[] {
169	  const rawToolCalls = Array.isArray((responseMessage as { tool_calls?: unknown[] }).tool_calls)
170	    ? (responseMessage as { tool_calls: unknown[] }).tool_calls
171	    : [];
172	  const structuredToolCalls = rawToolCalls.flatMap((toolCall) => {
173	    if (!isRecord(toolCall)) return [];
174	    const fn = isRecord(toolCall.function) ? toolCall.function : undefined;
175	    if (!fn || typeof fn.name !== "string" || !fn.name.trim()) return [];
176	    const { args, error } = parseToolArgsSafely(typeof fn.arguments === "string" ? fn.arguments : "{}");
177	    return [{
178	      id: typeof toolCall.id === "string" && toolCall.id.trim() ? toolCall.id : crypto.randomUUID(),
179	      name: fn.name,
180	      args,
181	      parseError: error,
182	    }];
183	  });
184	  if (structuredToolCalls.length > 0) return structuredToolCalls;
185	
186	  const text = extractAssistantText(responseMessage);
187	  if (!text) return [];
188	
189	  const textFormattedToolCalls = extractTextFormattedToolCalls(text);
190	  if (textFormattedToolCalls.length > 0) return textFormattedToolCalls;
191	
192	  const toolCalls: ExtractedToolCall[] = [];
193	  const runRe = /run\s*\(\s*(?:command\s*=\s*)?["']([^"']+)["']\s*\)/g;
194	  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
195	  while ((match = runRe.exec(text)) !== null) {
196	    toolCalls.push({
197	      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
198	      name: "run",
199	      args: { command: cleanCommand(match[1]) },
200	      parseError: null,
201	    });
202	  }
203	  if (toolCalls.length > 0) return toolCalls;
204	
205	  for (const line of text.split("\n")) {
206	    const stripped = line.replace(/^\s*\d+[.):-]\s*/, "").trim();
207	    if (!stripped || !COMMAND_VERBS.test(stripped)) continue;
208	    toolCalls.push({
209	      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
210	      name: "run",
211	      args: { command: cleanCommand(stripped) },
212	      parseError: null,
213	    });
214	  }
215	  if (toolCalls.length > 0) return toolCalls;
216	
217	  const patterns = [
218	    /Tool call run:\s*\n?\{?\s*"?command"?\s*[:=]\s*"([^"]+)"/,
219	    /<parameter\s+name="command">([^<]+)<\/parameter>/,
220	    /\[TOOL_CALL\][\s\S]*?--command\s+"([^"]+)"[\s\S]*?\[\/TOOL_CALL\]/,
221	    /command["\s:=]+["']([^"']+)["']/i,
222	  ];
223	  for (const pattern of patterns) {
224	    const inlineMatch = text.match(pattern);
225	    if (!inlineMatch) continue;
226	    return [{
227	      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
228	      name: "run",
229	      args: { command: cleanCommand(inlineMatch[1]) },
230	      parseError: null,
231	    }];
232	  }
233	
234	  return [];
235	}
236	
237	export function isToolError(result: string): boolean {
238	  return result.includes("not found")
239	    || result.includes("does not exist")
240	    || result.includes("requires")
241	    || result.includes("must be")
242	    || result.includes("Failed")
243	    || result.includes("Unknown command")
244	    || result.includes("Unknown tool")
245	    || result.includes("error");
246	}
247

87	function normalizeTimelinePlacement(value: unknown): TimelinePlacement | null {

89	    return null;

95	  const insertionTime = typeof value.insertion_time === "number" && Number.isFinite(value.insertion_time)
96	    ? value.insertion_time
97	    : undefined;
98	  const intent = value.intent === "after_source" || value.intent === "replace"
99	    ? value.intent

102	  if (!timelineId || !sourceClipId || !targetTrack || insertionTime === undefined || !intent) {

105	
106	  return {
107	    timeline_id: timelineId,
108	    source_clip_id: sourceClipId,
109	    target_track: targetTrack,
110	    insertion_time: insertionTime,
111	    intent,
112	  };
113	}

321	function buildCreateTaskRequest(args: CreateGenerationTaskArgs): CreateTaskRequest | null {
322	  const taskType = asTrimmedString(args.task_type) ?? "image_generation";
323	  switch (taskType) {
324	    case "image-to-video": {
325	      const input = buildTravelBetweenImagesInput(args);
326	      return input ? { family: "travel_between_images", input } : null;
327	    }
328	    case "image-to-image": {
329	      const input = buildZImageI2IInput(args);
330	      return input ? { family: "z_image_turbo_i2i", input } : null;
331	    }
332	    case "magic-edit": {
333	      const input = buildMagicEditInput(args);
334	      return input ? { family: "magic_edit", input } : null;
335	    }
336	    case "image-upscale": {
337	      const input = buildImageUpscaleInput(args);
338	      return input ? { family: "image_upscale", input } : null;
339	    }
340	    case "video-enhance": {
341	      const input = buildVideoEnhanceInput(args);
342	      return input ? { family: "video_enhance", input } : null;
343	    }
344	    case "character-animate": {
345	      const input = buildCharacterAnimateInput(args);
346	      return input ? { family: "character_animate", input } : null;
347	    }
348	    default: {
349	      const input = buildImageGenerationInput(args);
350	      return input ? { family: "image_generation", input } : null;
351	    }
352	  }
353	}

355	function requireEnv(name: string): string {
356	  const value = Deno.env.get(name);
357	  if (!value) {
358	    throw new Error(`[ai-timeline-agent] Missing ${name}`);
359	  }
360	  return value;
361	}
362	
363	async function sha256Hex(value: string): Promise<string> {
364	  const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(value);
365	  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", encoded);
366	  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest))
367	    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
368	    .join("");
369	}
370	
371	async function getIdempotencyKey(args: CreateGenerationTaskArgs): Promise<string> {
372	  if (typeof args.idempotency_key === "string" && args.idempotency_key.trim()) {
373	    return args.idempotency_key.trim();
374	  }
375	
376	  const payload = JSON.stringify({
377	    project_id: args.project_id,
378	    request: buildCreateTaskRequest(args),
379	  });
380	  const digest = await sha256Hex(payload);
381	  return `timeline-agent:${digest.slice(0, 40)}`;
382	}
383	
384	export async function createGenerationTask(args: CreateGenerationTaskArgs): Promise<ToolResult> {
385	  if (typeof args.project_id !== "string" || !args.project_id.trim()) {
386	    return { result: "create_generation_task requires project_id." };
387	  }
388	
389	  const request = buildCreateTaskRequest(args);
390	  if (!request) {
391	    return { result: "create_generation_task requires the required inputs for the selected task type." };
392	  }
393	
394	  const supabaseUrl = requireEnv("SUPABASE_URL");
395	  const serviceRoleKey = requireEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY");
396	  const idempotencyKey = await getIdempotencyKey(args);
397	
398	  const response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/create-task`, {
399	    method: "POST",
400	    headers: {
401	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
402	      Authorization: `Bearer ${serviceRoleKey}`,
403	      apikey: serviceRoleKey,
404	    },
405	    body: JSON.stringify({
406	      family: request.family,
407	      input: request.input,
408	      project_id: args.project_id.trim(),
409	      dependant_on: null,
410	      idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,
411	    }),
412	  });
413	
414	  if (!response.ok) {
415	    const errorText = await response.text().catch(() => "");
416	    const message = errorText || "Failed to create task";
417	    return { result: `Failed to create task: ${message}` };
418	  }
419	
420	  const data = await response.json() as { task_id?: string; deduplicated?: boolean };
421	  const taskLabel = TASK_LABELS[asTrimmedString(args.task_type) ?? ""]
422	    ?? TASK_LABELS[request.family]
423	    ?? request.family;
424	
425	  return {
426	    result: data.task_id
427	      ? `Queued ${taskLabel} task ${data.task_id}${data.deduplicated ? " (deduplicated)." : "."}`
428	      : `Queued ${taskLabel} task.`,
429	  };
430	}
431	
432	export const handlers: Record<string, ToolHandler> = {
433	  create_generation_task: (args) => createGenerationTask(args),
434	};

169	Use create_task({...}) for generation tasks. Copy selected clip URLs exactly into reference_image_urls or video_url.
170	If a selected clip line includes placement_anchor={...} and the user explicitly asks to insert the generated result after or in place of that source clip, copy that exact object into create_task.timeline_placement.
171	Do not add timeline_placement unless the user explicitly asks for edit-and-insert, place-after-source, or replace-on-timeline behavior.
172	Use transform_image({...}) for exact geometric image edits on an existing image: flip/mirror, rotate, zoom, reposition. This is deterministic and should preserve the source image exactly.
173	For exact image transforms, do not use create_task image-to-image or magic-edit.
174	By default, transform_image should create a new variant and make it the primary variant unless the user explicitly asks not to, or asks for a standalone new image.
175	When the user asks for multiple images, use count to request them all at once — the system automatically generates varied prompts for each.
176	Use get_tasks({}) to check on recent task status, errors, or results. Use get_tasks({"task_id":"..."}) for a specific task.
177	Use duplicate_generation({"generation_id":"..."}) to copy an existing generation instantly when the user wants a non-destructive derivative or alternate edit path.
178	For image-to-image and magic-edit requests against a selected image, default to creating a variant on that selected generation and make that new variant primary unless the user explicitly asks otherwise.
179	Do not set as_new:true unless the user explicitly asks for a standalone, separate, detached, or brand-new branch that should not stay attached to the source image lineage.
180	When a selected image is present, decide whether the user wants it used as actual input (reference image) or is just using it as context/inspiration. Consider the full request:
181	- **style-transfer**: The user wants the visual style/aesthetic applied to new content — "in this style", "use this look", "match this aesthetic". Use with reference_image_urls.
182	- **scene-transfer**: The user wants to keep the scene/environment/setting and generate new content within it — "in this scene", "in this setting", "in this environment", "same background", "same location". Use with reference_image_urls.
183	- **subject-transfer**: The user wants to keep the subject/character and place it in a new context — "of it", "same subject", "this character in a new scene". Use with reference_image_urls.
184	- **image-to-image**: The user wants to directly transform or edit the image — "edit this", "new angle of this", "make this more X". Use with reference_image_urls.
185	- **text-to-image**: The user is just drawing inspiration, not using the image as input — "with prompts like this", "similar concept", or they specify a model. Do not force a reference-based task type just because an image happens to be selected.
186	Key signals: "this style/look" → style-transfer. "This scene/setting/environment" → scene-transfer. "This subject/character" → subject-transfer. Specifies a model or talks about prompts → text-to-image. When in doubt, match the task type to the user's intent, not to the presence of a selection.
187	On follow-ups like "more like that", "again but...", "same thing but...", reuse the reference_image_urls from your previous create_task call — do not drop the reference just because no image is currently selected. The conversation history has the URL.
188	If the selected image only has placeholder metadata such as prompt="Uploaded ...", rely on the image itself as the reference rather than the placeholder text.
189	For style, subject, style-character, or scene transfer with multiple selections, choose the strongest matching reference instead of guessing.
190	For image-to-video or travel-between-images requests, use all selected reference_image_urls in the order that best matches the requested motion.
191	When a selected clip includes prompt="...", treat it as source metadata and reuse it instead of re-describing the image.
192	Duplicate & place workflow (multi-step — call each tool in order):
193	Step 1: duplicate_generation({"generation_id":"<id>"}) → returns new_generation_id, asset URL, type
194	Step 2 (optional edits): create_task({..., "based_on":"<new_generation_id>", "reference_image_urls":["<asset URL>"]})
195	Step 3 (place on timeline): run(command="add-media <track> <at> <new_generation_id> <asset_url> [--type video]")
196	When user says "duplicate and add after this clip", do BOTH step 1 and step 3. Calculate <at> = clip's at + clip's duration.
197	When user says just "duplicate", do only step 1.
198	Timeline insert guide:
199	- add-media <track> <at> <generation_id> <url> [--type video]
200	- default media type is image when --type is omitted

146	async function expandPrompts(basePrompt: string, count: number): Promise<string[]> {
147	  try {
148	    const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL");
149	    const serviceRoleKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY");
150	    if (!supabaseUrl || !serviceRoleKey) return numberedFallback(basePrompt, count);
151	
152	    const response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/ai-prompt`, {
153	      method: "POST",
154	      headers: {
155	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
156	        Authorization: `Bearer ${serviceRoleKey}`,
157	        apikey: serviceRoleKey,
158	      },
159	      body: JSON.stringify({
160	        task: "generate_prompts",
161	        overallPromptText: basePrompt,
162	        numberToGenerate: count,
163	        existingPrompts: [],
164	        temperature: 0.9,
165	      }),
166	    });
167	    if (!response.ok) return numberedFallback(basePrompt, count);
168	    const data = await response.json() as { prompts?: string[] };
169	    if (Array.isArray(data.prompts) && data.prompts.length > 0) {
170	      const prompts = data.prompts.slice(0, count);
171	      while (prompts.length < count) prompts.push(basePrompt);
172	      return prompts;
173	    }
174	  } catch {
175	    // Fall back
176	  }
177	  return numberedFallback(basePrompt, count);
178	}

183	  );
184	}

190	function ensureDistinctPrompts(basePrompt: string, prompts: string[], count: number): string[] {
191	  const uniquePrompts: string[] = [];
192	  const seen = new Set<string>();
193	
194	  for (const candidate of prompts) {
195	    const prompt = candidate.trim();
196	    if (!prompt) {
197	      continue;
198	    }
199	
200	    const [REDACTED](prompt);
201	    if (seen.has(key)) {
202	      continue;
203	    }
204	
205	    seen.add(key);
206	    uniquePrompts.push(prompt);
207	
208	    if (uniquePrompts.length >= count) {
209	      return uniquePrompts;
210	    }
211	  }
212	
213	  let fallbackIndex = 1;
214	  while (uniquePrompts.length < count) {
215	    const fallbackPrompt = numberedFallback(basePrompt, count + fallbackIndex)[count + fallbackIndex - 1];
216	    fallbackIndex += 1;
217	
218	    const [REDACTED](fallbackPrompt);
219	    if (seen.has(key)) {
220	      continue;
221	    }
222	
223	    seen.add(key);
224	    uniquePrompts.push(fallbackPrompt);
225	  }
226	
227	  return uniquePrompts;
228	}

230	async function sha256Hex(value: string): Promise<string> {
231	  const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(value);
232	  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", encoded);
233	  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest))
234	    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
235	    .join("");
236	}

243	  }
244	  return batchTimestamp;
245	}

> AGENT

Perfect! Now I have the complete picture of the bug. Here's my findings:

## Bug Report: Timeline Agent - Identical Prompts in Batch Generation

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.ts`, **lines 456-513**

**Root Cause:** The batch prompt expansion logic only triggers when `count > 1` is passed to a single `create_task()` call. If the LLM model calls `create_task()` 20 times with `count: 1` each (instead of 1 call with `count: 20`), each task gets the exact same prompt.

**The Specific Logic Flow:**
- **Line 456**: Batch expansion only activates: `if (requestedCount > 1 && prompt)`
- **Lines 458-462**: Calls `expandPrompts(prompt, targetCount)` which hits the ai-prompt edge function to generate varied prompts
- **Line 470**: Each batch task gets `count: 1` (individual tasks, not a batch)
- **Lines 515-533**: If batch expansion doesn't trigger (count=1), it skips prompt variation entirely and reuses the same prompt

**Why 20 Identical Images Appear:**
When user says "Make more with z-image please", the agent likely misinterprets this and makes 20 separate tool calls like:
```
create_task({task_type: "text-to-image", prompt: "original prompt", count: 1})
```
instead of:
```
create_task({task_type: "text-to-image", prompt: "original prompt", count: 20})
```

Each individual call skips the batch expansion (line 456 condition fails), so line 517 passes the original prompt unchanged to `createGenerationTask()` 20 times.

**The System Prompt Instruction (line 175)** explicitly tells the LLM to use `count` parameter: "When the user asks for multiple images, use count to request them all at once — the system automatically generates varied prompts for each." But the LLM is not following this instruction correctly.

> DEVELOPER

Design the implementation plan for a variation-intent feature in Reigh's ai-prompt / ai-timeline-agent system. This is a design task — I want tradeoffs and a recommendation, not code.

## Context

When a user chats with the timeline agent (reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/) and asks "make 10 more with z-image", the agent calls its `create_task` tool with `count: 10`. The tool's batch path (create-task.ts:456 in `executeCreateTask`) calls `expandPrompts` (create-task.ts:146), which POSTs to the `ai-prompt` edge function (reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-prompt/index.ts, case `generate_prompts`) asking for N varied prompts.

The bug we just diagnosed: Kimi K2 (moonshotai/kimi-k2-instruct-0905) in ai-prompt is returning 10 verbatim copies of the user's base prompt instead of 10 variations. The template (ai-prompt/templates.ts:8 buildGeneratePromptsMessages) says "Generate exactly N distinct prompts" but also "keep the user's descriptions as stated — only add detail when specifically requested". Kimi resolves that conflict by echoing the input N times. Then `ensureDistinctPrompts` (create-task.ts:190) dedups and pads with `numberedFallback` (adds "(variation 11)...(variation 19)" suffix), so the GPU model sees 10 near-identical prompts and produces near-identical images.

## User's direction for the fix

> "The variance should be based on the user's query — not hard-coded axes. The default should be to say the same thing in X different ways with different characters, but it should vary based on what the user requests."

So:
- Default behavior: same subject/scene, same core idea, expressed in N different ways. Characters/people can vary. Don't force camera-angle/lighting axes unless the user asked for that.
- When the user says "try different lighting" / "different angles" / "with different characters" — the variation axis follows their intent.

The timeline agent's LLM has the full chat history and naturally knows the user's intent. `ai-prompt` currently does not — it only receives `overallPromptText` (the base prompt).

## Questions I need your plan to answer

1. **Where should "variation intent" be derived?** Two options:
   a. The timeline agent LLM decides — add an optional `variation_intent` arg to the `create_task` tool schema. The model fills it based on the user's conversation. Pass it through `executeCreateTask` → `expandPrompts` → `ai-prompt` POST body → the ai-prompt template.
   b. `ai-prompt` derives it itself — but it has no access to the user's conversation, so this doesn't work unless we also pass chat history.
   Option (a) seems obviously better — confirm or argue otherwise.

2. **Template redesign for `buildGeneratePromptsMessages`**: write the new system+user prompt that gets the default behavior right ("same thing, N ways") when no intent is given, and follows the intent when one is given. Include the specific wording, and explain why Kimi K2 will respect it where the current template doesn't.

3. **Should we also switch models?** Kimi K2 has a track record here of echoing input. Is it worth using a different Groq model, or Claude Haiku 4.5 via Anthropic? Factor in: latency (this blocks the user), cost (batched per expandPrompts call), reliability at following "N distinct outputs" instructions. One-sentence rec.

4. **Existing call sites of `ai-prompt`'s `generate_prompts`**: are there OTHER callers beyond the timeline agent that would be affected by adding a new optional field? Search `reigh-app/` for `task: "generate_prompts"` or `/functions/v1/ai-prompt`. If there are, note them so the contract change is safe.

5. **Schema shape**: `variation_intent` as a string is flexible but underspecified. Should it be freeform text the LLM writes ("user wants different camera angles")? Or a structured enum? Or both (freeform + optional tags)? Recommend one.

6. **Fallback behavior**: today when ai-prompt fails, `numberedFallback` adds " (variation N)" suffixes — useless for image generation. What should the new fallback be? (Options: fail loudly and tell the user; generate local variations by prepending style tokens; retry with a different model.) Pick one with justification.

## Output format

Return:
- **Recommendation section** — the answer to each of the 6 questions, 2-3 sentences each.
- **Step-by-step plan** — ordered list of file-level changes with specific function signatures / template text. Skip obvious boilerplate; focus on the decisions.
- **Risks / open questions** — anything I should verify or ask the user about before implementing.

Under 800 words total. This is for a senior engineer implementing the change; they know the codebase — don't over-explain mechanics they can see from the file paths.

93	  // Rate limit by user ID (skip for service role — internal agent calls)
94	  if (auth.userId) {
95	    const rateLimitDenied = await enforceRateLimit({
96	      supabaseAdmin,
97	      functionName: 'ai-prompt',
98	      userId: auth.userId,
99	      config: RATE_LIMITS.expensive,
100	      logger,
101	      logPrefix: '[AI-PROMPT]',
102	      responses: {
103	        serviceUnavailable: () => jsonResponse({ error: 'Rate limit service unavailable' }, 503),
104	      },
105	    });
106	    if (rateLimitDenied) {
107	      return rateLimitDenied;
108	    }
109	  }
110	
111	  const task = body.task as string | undefined;
112	  if (!task) return jsonResponse({ error: "task is required" }, 400);
113	
114	  try {
115	    switch (task) {
116	      case "generate_prompts": {
117	        const groq = getGroqClient();
118	        const overallPromptText = String(body.overallPromptText ?? "");
119	        const rulesToRememberText = String(body.rulesToRememberText ?? "");
120	        const numberToGenerate = Number(body.numberToGenerate ?? 3);
121	        const existingPrompts = Array.isArray(body.existingPrompts) ? body.existingPrompts as unknown[] : [];
122	        const temperature = Number(body.temperature ?? 0.8);
123	
124	        const { systemMsg, userMsg } = buildGeneratePromptsMessages({
125	          overallPromptText,
126	          rulesToRememberText,
127	          numberToGenerate,
128	          existingPrompts,
129	        });
130	
131	        logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_prompts: starting Groq call, model=${GROQ_MODEL}, numberToGenerate=${numberToGenerate}`);
132	        const groqStart = Date.now();
133	        const resp = await groqWithFallback(groq, {
134	          model: GROQ_MODEL,
135	          messages: [
136	            { role: "system", content: systemMsg },
137	            { role: "user", content: userMsg },
138	          ],
139	          temperature: temperature,
140	          max_tokens: 4096,
141	          top_p: 1,
142	        }, logger);
143	        logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_prompts: Groq call completed in ${Date.now() - groqStart}ms, model=${resp.model}`);
144	        const outputText = resp.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || "";
145	        const prompts = outputText.split("\n").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
146	        
147	        // Validate we got the expected number of prompts
148	        if (prompts.length !== numberToGenerate) {
149	          // If we got too many, take the first N
150	          if (prompts.length > numberToGenerate) {
151	            prompts.splice(numberToGenerate);
152	          }
153	          // If we got too few, we'll just return what we have rather than failing
154	        }
155	        
156	        return jsonResponse({ prompts, usage: resp.usage });
157	      }
158	      case "edit_prompt": {
159	        const groq = getGroqClient();
160	        const originalPromptText = String(body.originalPromptText ?? "");
161	        const editInstructions = String(body.editInstructions ?? "");
162	        if (!originalPromptText || !editInstructions) return jsonResponse({ error: "originalPromptText and editInstructions required" }, 400);
163	        const { systemMsg, userMsg } = buildEditPromptMessages({
164	          originalPromptText,
165	          editInstructions,
166	        });
167	        const resp = await groqWithFallback(groq, {
168	          model: GROQ_MODEL,
169	          messages: [
170	            { role: "system", content: systemMsg },
171	            { role: "user", content: userMsg },
172	          ],
173	          temperature: 0.7,
174	          max_tokens: 2048,
175	          top_p: 1,
176	        }, logger);
177	        const newText = resp.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || originalPromptText;
178	        return jsonResponse({ success: true, newText, usage: resp.usage });
179	      }
180	      case "generate_summary": {
181	        const groq = getGroqClient();
182	        const promptText = String(body.promptText ?? "");
183	        if (!promptText) return jsonResponse({ error: "promptText required" }, 400);
184	        logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_summary: starting Groq call`);
185	        const summaryStart = Date.now();
186	        const resp = await groqWithFallback(groq, {
187	          model: GROQ_MODEL,
188	          messages: [{ role: "user", content: `Create a brief summary of this image prompt in 10 words or less. Output only the summary text with no additional formatting or quotation marks:
189	
190	"${promptText}"
191	
192	Summary:` }],
193	          temperature: 1.0,
194	          max_tokens: 50,
195	          top_p: 1,
196	        }, logger);
197	        logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_summary: Groq call completed in ${Date.now() - summaryStart}ms`);
198	        const summary = resp.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || null;
199	        return jsonResponse({ summary, usage: resp.usage });
200	      }
201	      case "enhance_segment_prompt": {
202	        // Enhance segment prompt(s) using OpenAI GPT-5 Mini
203	        // Accepts either a single `prompt` string or an array `prompts` for batch processing.
204	        const isBatch = Array.isArray(body.prompts);
205	        const prompts: string[] = isBatch
206	          ? (body.prompts as string[]).map((p: unknown) => String(p ?? ""))
207	          : [String(body.prompt ?? "")];
208	
209	        if (!isBatch && !prompts[0].trim()) {
210	          return jsonResponse({ error: "prompt is required" }, 400);
211	        }
212	
213	        const openaiApiKey = getOpenAIApiKey();
214	        const systemMsg = ENHANCE_SEGMENT_SYSTEM_PROMPT;
215	
216	        const MAX_RETRIES = 2;
217	        const RETRY_DELAY_MS = 2000;
218	
219	        async function callOpenAI(p: string): Promise<Response> {
220	          return fetch("https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions", {
221	            method: "POST",
222	            headers: {
223	              "Authorization": `Bearer ${openaiApiKey}`,
224	              "Content-Type": "application/json",
225	            },
226	            body: JSON.stringify({
227	              model: "gpt-5-mini",
228	              messages: [
229	                { role: "system", content: systemMsg },
230	                { role: "user", content: buildEnhanceSegmentUserPrompt(p) },
231	              ],
232	              max_completion_tokens: 16000,
233	            }),
234	          });
235	        }
236	
237	        function delay(ms: number): Promise<void> {
238	          return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
239	        }
240	
241	        async function enhanceOne(p: string): Promise<{ enhanced_prompt: string; usage?: unknown }> {
242	          if (!p.trim()) return { enhanced_prompt: "" };
243	
244	          for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= MAX_RETRIES; attempt++) {
245	            try {
246	              const response = await callOpenAI(p);
247	
248	              if (response.status === 429) {
249	                console.warn(`[ai-prompt] OpenAI 429, attempt ${attempt + 1}/${MAX_RETRIES + 1}`);
250	                if (attempt < MAX_RETRIES) {
251	                  await delay(RETRY_DELAY_MS * (attempt + 1));
252	                  continue;
253	                }
254	                return { enhanced_prompt: p };
255	              }
256	
257	              if (!response.ok) {
258	                const errorText = await response.text();
259	                console.error(`[ai-prompt] OpenAI API error:`, response.status, errorText);
260	                return { enhanced_prompt: p };
261	              }
262	
263	              const data = await response.json();
264	              return {
265	                enhanced_prompt: data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || p,
266	                usage: data.usage,
267	              };
268	            } catch (err) {
269	              console.error(`[ai-prompt] OpenAI fetch error attempt ${attempt + 1}:`, toErrorMessage(err));
270	              if (attempt < MAX_RETRIES) {
271	                await delay(RETRY_DELAY_MS * (attempt + 1));
272	                continue;
273	              }
274	              return { enhanced_prompt: p };
275	            }
276	          }
277	
278	          return { enhanced_prompt: p };
279	        }
280	
281	        try {
282	          if (!isBatch) {
283	            const result = await enhanceOne(prompts[0]);
284	            return jsonResponse(result);
285	          }
286	
287	          // Batch: fire all concurrently server-side
288	          const results = await Promise.all(prompts.map(enhanceOne));
289	          return jsonResponse({
290	            enhanced_prompts: results.map(r => r.enhanced_prompt),
291	            usage: results.map(r => r.usage).filter(Boolean),
292	          });
293	        } catch (fetchError: unknown) {
294	          const message = toErrorMessage(fetchError);
295	          console.error(`[ai-prompt] OpenAI fetch error:`, message);
296	          return jsonResponse({ error: "Failed to call OpenAI API", details: message }, 500);
297	        }
298	      }
299	      default:
300	        return jsonResponse({ error: `Unknown task: ${task}` }, 400);
301	    }
302	  } catch (err: unknown) {
303	    const message = toErrorMessage(err);
304	    console.error(`[ai-prompt] Error handling task ${task}:`, message);
305	    return jsonResponse({ error: "Internal server error", details: message }, 500);
306	  }
307	}); 
308
