```
16	  info?: (message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
17	  warn?: (message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
18	  error?: (message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
19	};
20	
21	const APPEND_SHOT_POSITION = 2_147_483_647;
22	const MAX_BATCH_VARIATIONS = 16;
23	const SUPPORTED_CREATE_TASK_TYPES = new Set([
24	  "text-to-image",
25	  "style-transfer",
26	  "subject-transfer",
27	  "style-character-transfer",
28	  "scene-transfer",
29	  "image-to-video",
30	  "image-to-image",
31	  "magic-edit",
32	  "image-upscale",
33	  "video-enhance",
34	  "character-animate",
35	]);
36	const TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE: Record<string, string> = {
37	  "style-transfer": "style",
38	  "subject-transfer": "subject",
39	  "style-character-transfer": "style-character",
40	  "scene-transfer": "scene",
41	};
42	const TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_PROMPT = new Set([
43	  "text-to-image",
44	  "style-transfer",
45	  "subject-transfer",
46	  "style-character-transfer",
47	  "scene-transfer",
48	  "image-to-video",
49	  "image-to-image",
50	  "magic-edit",
51	]);
52	const TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_REFERENCE_IMAGE = new Set([
53	  "style-transfer",
54	  "subject-transfer",
55	  "style-character-transfer",
56	  "scene-transfer",
57	  "image-to-image",
58	  "magic-edit",
59	  "image-upscale",
60	  "character-animate",
61	]);
62	const TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_VIDEO = new Set([
63	  "video-enhance",
64	  "character-animate",
65	]);
66	const TASK_TYPES_SUPPORTING_SOURCE_PLACEMENT = new Set([
67	  "image-to-image",
68	  "magic-edit",
69	  "image-upscale",
70	]);
71	
72	function asFiniteNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
73	  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
74	}
75	
76	function normalizeTimelinePlacementArg(value: unknown): TimelinePlacement | undefined {
77	  if (!isRecord(value)) {
78	    return undefined;
79	  }
80	
81	  const timelineId = asTrimmedString(value.timeline_id);
82	  const sourceClipId = asTrimmedString(value.source_clip_id);
83	  const targetTrack = asTrimmedString(value.target_track);
84	  const insertionTime = typeof value.insertion_time === "number" && Number.isFinite(value.insertion_time)
85	    ? value.insertion_time
86	    : undefined;
87	  const intent = value.intent === "after_source" || value.intent === "replace"
88	    ? value.intent
89	    : undefined;
90	
91	  if (!timelineId || !sourceClipId || !targetTrack || insertionTime === undefined || !intent) {
92	    return undefined;
93	  }
94	
95	  return {
96	    timeline_id: timelineId,
97	    source_clip_id: sourceClipId,
98	    target_track: targetTrack,
99	    insertion_time: insertionTime,
100	    intent,
101	  };
102	}
103	
104	function buildPlacementIntent(
105	  taskType: string,
106	  resolvedSelectionContexts: ReturnType<typeof resolveSelectionContext>,
107	): PlacementIntent | undefined {
108	  if (!TASK_TYPES_SUPPORTING_SOURCE_PLACEMENT.has(taskType)) {
109	    return undefined;
110	  }
111	
112	  const timelineSelections = resolvedSelectionContexts.filter((context) => context.is_on_timeline);
113	  if (timelineSelections.length !== 1) {
114	    return undefined;
115	  }
116	
117	  const anchor = timelineSelections[0];
118	  return {
119	    timeline_id: anchor.timeline_id,
120	    anchor_clip_id: anchor.clip_id,
121	    ...(anchor.generation_id ? { anchor_generation_id: anchor.generation_id } : {}),
122	    ...(anchor.variant_id ? { anchor_variant_id: anchor.variant_id } : {}),
123	    relation: "after",
124	    preferred_track_id: anchor.track_id,
125	    fallback_at: anchor.at + anchor.duration,
126	    fallback_track_id: anchor.track_id,
127	  };
128	}
129	
130	function withPlacementIntent(
131	  params: Record<string, unknown> | undefined,
132	  placementIntent: PlacementIntent | undefined,
133	): Record<string, unknown> | undefined {
134	  if (!placementIntent) {
135	    return params;
136	  }
137	
138	  return {
139	    ...(params ?? {}),
140	    placement_intent: placementIntent,
141	  };
142	}
143	
144	// Default reference strength params per mode — matches the form's REFERENCE_MODE_DEFAULTS for qwen-image
145	const REFERENCE_MODE_DEFAULTS: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>> = {
146	  style: { style_reference_strength: 1.1 },
147	  subject: { style_reference_strength: 0.4, subject_strength: 1.0 },
148	  "style-character": { style_reference_strength: 0.4, subject_strength: 1.0 },
149	  scene: { style_reference_strength: 0.4, in_this_scene: true, in_this_scene_strength: 1.0 },
150	};
151	
152	async function expandPrompts(
153	  basePrompt: string,
154	  count: number,
155	  logger?: ExpandPromptsLogger,
156	): Promise<string[]> {
157	  const logError = (message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) => {
158	    if (logger?.error) logger.error(message, context);
159	    else console.error(message, context ?? "");
160	  };
161	  const logWarn = (message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) => {
162	    if (logger?.warn) logger.warn(message, context);
163	    else console.warn(message, context ?? "");
164	  };
165	  if (logger?.info) {
166	    logger.info("[agent] expandPrompts: called", { count, loggerPresent: true });
167	  } else {
168	    console.log("[agent] expandPrompts: called (no logger)", { count });
169	  }
170	
171	  try {
172	    const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL");
173	    const serviceRoleKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY");
174	    if (!supabaseUrl || !serviceRoleKey) {
175	      logError("[agent] expandPrompts: missing SUPABASE_URL or SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, using fallback");
176	      return numberedFallback(basePrompt, count);
177	    }
178	
179	    const response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/ai-prompt`, {
180	      method: "POST",
181	      headers: {
182	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
183	        Authorization: `Bearer ${serviceRoleKey}`,
184	        apikey: serviceRoleKey,
185	      },
186	      body: JSON.stringify({
187	        task: "generate_prompts",
188	        overallPromptText: basePrompt,
189	        numberToGenerate: count,
190	        existingPrompts: [],
191	        temperature: 0.9,
192	      }),
193	    });
194	    if (!response.ok) {
195	      const body = await response.text().catch(() => "<unreadable>");
196	      logError("[agent] expandPrompts: ai-prompt HTTP error, using fallback", {
197	        status: response.status,
198	        statusText: response.statusText,
199	        body: body.slice(0, 500),
200	        count,
201	      });
202	      return numberedFallback(basePrompt, count);
203	    }
204	    const data = await response.json() as { prompts?: string[] };
205	    if (Array.isArray(data.prompts) && data.prompts.length > 0) {
206	      const prompts = data.prompts.slice(0, count);
207	      const uniqueNormalized = new Set(
208	        prompts.map((p) => p.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().toLowerCase()),
209	      );
210	      if (logger?.info) {
211	        logger.info("[agent] expandPrompts: ai-prompt returned", {
212	          requested: count,
213	          returned: data.prompts.length,
214	          uniqueNormalized: uniqueNormalized.size,
215	          firstTwoHeads: prompts.slice(0, 2).map((p) => p.slice(0, 120)),
216	        });
217	      }
218	      if (prompts.length < count) {
219	        logWarn("[agent] expandPrompts: ai-prompt returned partial result, padding with basePrompt", {
220	          returned: prompts.length,
221	          requested: count,
222	        });
223	      }
224	      while (prompts.length < count) prompts.push(basePrompt);
225	      return prompts;
226	    }
227	    logError("[agent] expandPrompts: ai-prompt returned no prompts, using fallback", {
228	      responseKeys: Object.keys(data ?? {}),
229	      count,
230	    });
231	  } catch (err) {
232	    const message = err instanceof Error ? `${err.name}: ${err.message}` : String(err);
233	    logError("[agent] expandPrompts: threw, using fallback", { error: message, count });
234	  }
235	  return numberedFallback(basePrompt, count);
236	}
237	
238	function numberedFallback(basePrompt: string, count: number): string[] {
239	  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) =>
240	    i === 0 ? basePrompt : `${basePrompt} (variation ${i + 1})`
241	  );
242	}
243	
244	function normalizePromptKey(prompt: string): string {
245	  return prompt.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().toLowerCase();
246	}
247	
248	function ensureDistinctPrompts(basePrompt: string, prompts: string[], count: number): string[] {
249	  const uniquePrompts: string[] = [];
250	  const seen = new Set<string>();
251	
252	  for (const candidate of prompts) {
253	    const prompt = candidate.trim();
254	    if (!prompt) {
255	      continue;
256	    }
257	
258	    const [REDACTED](prompt);
259	    if (seen.has(key)) {
260	      continue;
261	    }
262	
263	    seen.add(key);
264	    uniquePrompts.push(prompt);
265	
266	    if (uniquePrompts.length >= count) {
267	      return uniquePrompts;
268	    }
269	  }
270	
271	  let fallbackIndex = 1;
272	  while (uniquePrompts.length < count) {
273	    const fallbackPrompt = numberedFallback(basePrompt, count + fallbackIndex)[count + fallbackIndex - 1];
274	    fallbackIndex += 1;
275	
276	    const [REDACTED](fallbackPrompt);
277	    if (seen.has(key)) {
278	      continue;
279	    }
280	
281	    seen.add(key);
282	    uniquePrompts.push(fallbackPrompt);
283	  }
284	
285	  return uniquePrompts;
286	}
287	
288	async function sha256Hex(value: string): Promise<string> {
289	  const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(value);
290	  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", encoded);
291	  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest))
292	    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
293	    .join("");
294	}
295	
296	// Batch timestamp ensures "10 more" creates new tasks instead of deduplicating against previous batches
297	let batchTimestamp: string | null = null;
298	function getBatchTimestamp(): string {
299	  if (!batchTimestamp) {
300	    batchTimestamp = Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);
301	  }
302	  return batchTimestamp;
303	}
304	
305	async function buildBatchIdempotencyKey(args: CreateGenerationTaskArgs, batchIndex: number): Promise<string> {
306	  const payload = JSON.stringify({
307	    project_id: args.project_id,
308	    request: {
309	      prompt: args.prompt,
310	      task_type: args.task_type,
311	      reference_mode: args.reference_mode,
312	      reference_image_url: args.reference_image_url,
313	      shot_id: args.shot_id,
314	      model_name: args.model_name,
315	      image_urls: args.image_urls,
316	      video_url: args.video_url,
317	      strength: args.strength,
318	      based_on: args.based_on,
319	      generation_id: args.generation_id,
320	      timeline_placement: args.timeline_placement,
321	      params: args.params,
322	      batch_index: batchIndex,
323	      batch_ts: getBatchTimestamp(),
324	    },
325	  });
326	  const digest = await sha256Hex(payload);
327	  return `timeline-agent:${digest.slice(0, 40)}`;
328	}
329	
330	export async function executeCreateTask(
331	  args: Record<string, unknown>,
332	  timelineState: TimelineState,
333	  selectedClips: SelectedClipPayload[] | undefined,
334	  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseAdmin,
335	  generationContext?: GenerationContext,
336	  timelineId = "",
337	  logger?: ExpandPromptsLogger,
338	): Promise<Pick<ToolResult, "result">> {
339	  const imageContext = generationContext?.image ?? null;
340	  const travelContext = generationContext?.travel ?? null;
341	  const taskType = asTrimmedString(args.task_type);
342	  const prompt = asTrimmedString(args.prompt);
343	  if (!taskType) {
344	    return { result: "create_task requires task_type." };
345	  }
346	  if (!SUPPORTED_CREATE_TASK_TYPES.has(taskType)) {
347	    return { result: `create_task does not support task_type ${taskType}.` };
348	  }
349	  if (TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_PROMPT.has(taskType) && !prompt) {
350	    return { result: `create_task ${taskType} requires prompt.` };
351	  }
352	
353	  const requestedReferenceImageUrls = asStringArray(args.reference_image_urls);
354	  const activeReference = imageContext?.activeReference ?? null;
355	  const shouldUseActiveReference = requestedReferenceImageUrls.length === 0
356	    && Boolean(TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE[taskType])
357	    && Boolean(activeReference?.url);
358	  const referenceImageUrls = shouldUseActiveReference && activeReference?.url
359	    ? [activeReference.url]
360	    : requestedReferenceImageUrls;
361	  const referenceMode = asTrimmedString(args.reference_mode) ?? TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE[taskType] ?? undefined;
362	  const videoUrl = asTrimmedString(args.video_url);
363	  const strength = typeof args.strength === "number" && Number.isFinite(args.strength) ? args.strength : undefined;
364	  if (strength !== undefined && (strength < 0 || strength > 1)) {
365	    return { result: "create_task strength must be between 0 and 1." };
366	  }
367	  const timelinePlacement = normalizeTimelinePlacementArg(args.timeline_placement);
368	  if (args.timeline_placement !== undefined && !timelinePlacement) {
369	    return {
370	      result: "create_task timeline_placement must include timeline_id, source_clip_id, target_track, insertion_time, and intent.",
371	    };
372	  }
373	  if (taskType === "image-to-video" && referenceImageUrls.length < 1) {
374	    return { result: "create_task image-to-video requires at least one reference_image_url." };
375	  }
376	  if (TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_REFERENCE_IMAGE.has(taskType) && referenceImageUrls.length === 0) {
377	    return { result: `create_task ${taskType} requires reference_image_urls.` };
378	  }
379	  if (TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_VIDEO.has(taskType) && !videoUrl) {
380	    return { result: `create_task ${taskType} requires video_url.` };
381	  }
382	
383	  const resolvedSelectionContexts = resolveSelectionContext(selectedClips ?? [], timelineState, timelineId);
384	  const selectedClipEntries = (selectedClips ?? []).map((clip, index) => ({
385	    clip,
386	    resolvedContext: resolvedSelectionContexts[index],
387	  }));
388	  const selectedReferenceEntries = selectedClipEntries.filter(({ clip }) => referenceImageUrls.includes(clip.url));
389	  const selectedVideoEntry = videoUrl
390	    ? selectedClipEntries.find(({ clip }) => clip.url === videoUrl)
391	    : undefined;
392	  const selectedEntriesForShot = selectedVideoEntry && !selectedReferenceEntries.some(({ clip }) => clip.url === selectedVideoEntry.clip.url)
393	    ? [...selectedReferenceEntries, selectedVideoEntry]
394	    : selectedReferenceEntries;
395	  const selectedClipsForShot = selectedEntriesForShot.map(({ clip }) => clip);
396	  const placementIntent = buildPlacementIntent(taskType, resolvedSelectionContexts);
397	  const generationIds = resolveClipGenerationIds(selectedClipsForShot, timelineState.registry, timelineState.config);
398	  let shotId = (
399	    await resolveSelectedClipShot(supabaseAdmin, timelineState, selectedClipsForShot)
400	  ).shotId;
401	  let shotNote = "";
402	  const shotName = asTrimmedString(args.shot_name);
403	  const needsAutoShot = !shotId && generationIds.length > 0 && taskType === "image-to-video";
404	  if (!shotId && (shotName || needsAutoShot) && generationIds.length > 0) {
405	    const effectiveShotName = shotName || `Shot ${new Date().toISOString().slice(11, 19)}`;
406	    shotId = await createShotWithGenerations(supabaseAdmin, {
407	      projectId: timelineState.projectId,
408	      shotName: effectiveShotName,
409	      generationIds,
410	      position: APPEND_SHOT_POSITION,
411	    });
412	    shotNote = ` Created shot ${effectiveShotName} (${shotId}).`;
413	  } else if (shotId) {
414	    shotNote = ` Reused shot ${shotId}.`;
415	  }
416	
417	  const asNew = args.as_new === true;
418	  const shouldDefaultPrimaryVariant = taskType === "image-to-image" || taskType === "magic-edit";
419	  const makePrimary = asNew
420	    ? undefined
421	    : typeof args.make_primary === "boolean"
422	      ? args.make_primary
423	      : shouldDefaultPrimaryVariant
424	        ? true
425	        : undefined;
426	  const basedOn = asNew
427	    ? undefined
428	    : (asTrimmedString(args.based_on) ?? (taskType === "video-enhance"
429	      ? selectedVideoEntry?.resolvedContext?.generation_id
430	      : selectedReferenceEntries[0]?.resolvedContext?.generation_id));
431	  const sourceVariantId = TASK_TYPES_SUPPORTING_SOURCE_PLACEMENT.has(taskType)
432	    ? (asTrimmedString(args.source_variant_id) ?? (taskType === "video-enhance"
433	      ? selectedVideoEntry?.resolvedContext?.variant_id
434	      : selectedReferenceEntries[0]?.resolvedContext?.variant_id))
435	    : asTrimmedString(args.source_variant_id);
436	  const generationId = taskType === "image-upscale"
437	    ? selectedReferenceEntries[0]?.resolvedContext?.generation_id
438	    : undefined;
439	  const defaultModelName = taskType === "image-to-video"
440	    ? travelContext?.selectedModel
441	    : (taskType === "text-to-image" || Boolean(TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE[taskType]))
442	      ? imageContext?.defaultModelName
443	      : undefined;
444	  const activeReferenceParams = shouldUseActiveReference && activeReference
445	    ? {
446	      ...(activeReference.styleReferenceStrength !== undefined
447	        ? { style_reference_strength: activeReference.styleReferenceStrength }
448	        : {}),
449	      ...(activeReference.subjectStrength !== undefined
450	        ? { subject_strength: activeReference.subjectStrength }
451	        : {}),
452	      ...(typeof activeReference.subjectDescription === "string" && activeReference.subjectDescription.trim()
453	        ? { subject_description: activeReference.subjectDescription }
454	        : {}),
455	      ...(activeReference.inThisScene !== undefined
456	        ? { in_this_scene: activeReference.inThisScene }
457	        : {}),
458	      ...(activeReference.inThisSceneStrength !== undefined
459	        ? { in_this_scene_strength: activeReference.inThisSceneStrength }
460	        : {}),
461	    }
462	    : undefined;
463	  const effectiveModel = referenceMode
464	    ? "qwen-image"
465	    : (asTrimmedString(args.model) ?? defaultModelName ?? undefined);
466	  const loraCategory = TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE[taskType]
467	    ? "qwen"
468	    : (effectiveModel?.startsWith("z-") ? "z-image" : "qwen");
469	  const loras = imageContext?.selectedLorasByCategory?.[loraCategory] ?? [];
470	  const rawMotion = asFiniteNumber(args.amount_of_motion);
471	  const travelParams = taskType === "image-to-video"
472	    ? {
473	      amount_of_motion: rawMotion !== null
474	        ? rawMotion / 100
475	        : (travelContext ? travelContext.amountOfMotion / 100 : undefined),
476	      steps: asPositiveNumber(args.steps) ?? travelContext?.steps,
477	      guidance_scale: asFiniteNumber(args.guidance_scale) ?? travelContext?.guidanceScale,
478	      enhance_prompt: typeof args.enhance_prompt === "boolean" ? args.enhance_prompt : travelContext?.enhancePrompt,
479	      turbo_mode: typeof args.turbo_mode === "boolean" ? args.turbo_mode : travelContext?.turboMode,
480	      loras: travelContext?.loras?.map((lora) => ({ path: lora.path, strength: lora.strength })),
481	      negative_prompts: travelContext?.negativePrompt ? [travelContext.negativePrompt] : undefined,
482	      text_before_prompts: travelContext?.textBeforePrompts || undefined,
483	      text_after_prompts: travelContext?.textAfterPrompts || undefined,
484	      segment_frames: travelContext ? [travelContext.frames] : undefined,
485	      model_type: travelContext?.generationTypeMode,
486	      generation_mode: travelContext ? (travelContext.generationMode ?? "timeline") : undefined,
487	      phase_config: travelContext?.phaseConfig,
488	    }
489	    : undefined;
490	  // For transfer tasks: start with mode defaults, then overlay active reference params (which may override)
491	  const modeDefaults = referenceMode ? (REFERENCE_MODE_DEFAULTS[referenceMode] ?? {}) : {};
492	  const mergedParams = {
493	    ...modeDefaults,
494	    ...(activeReferenceParams ?? {}),
495	    ...(loras.length > 0 ? { loras } : {}),
496	  };
497	  const filteredTravelParams = travelParams
498	    ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(travelParams).filter(([, value]) => value !== undefined))
499	    : undefined;
500	
501	  const requestedCount = asPositiveNumber(args.count) ?? 1;
502	  const taskParams = taskType === "image-to-video"
503	    ? (filteredTravelParams && Object.keys(filteredTravelParams).length > 0 ? filteredTravelParams : undefined)
504	    : (() => {
505	      const variantParams = {
506	        ...mergedParams,
507	        ...(makePrimary !== undefined ? { is_primary: makePrimary } : {}),
508	      };
509	      return Object.keys(variantParams).length > 0 ? variantParams : undefined;
510	    })();
511	  const taskParamsWithPlacement = withPlacementIntent(taskParams, placementIntent);
512	
513	  // When count > 1 and there's a prompt, expand into varied prompts and create separate tasks
514	  batchTimestamp = null; // Reset so each batch gets a unique timestamp
515	  if (requestedCount > 1 && prompt) {
516	    const targetCount = Math.min(requestedCount, MAX_BATCH_VARIATIONS);
517	    const expandedPrompts = ensureDistinctPrompts(
518	      prompt,
519	      await expandPrompts(prompt, targetCount, logger),
520	      targetCount,
521	    );
522	    let queuedCount = 0;
523	    let failedCount = 0;
524	    let deduplicatedCount = 0;
525	
526	    for (const [index, expandedPrompt] of expandedPrompts.entries()) {
527	      const generationArgs: CreateGenerationTaskArgs = {
528	        project_id: timelineState.projectId,
529	        prompt: expandedPrompt,
530	        count: 1,
531	        task_type: taskType,
532	        reference_mode: referenceMode,
533	        reference_image_url: referenceImageUrls[0],
534	        image_urls: taskType === "image-to-video" ? referenceImageUrls : undefined,
535	        video_url: videoUrl ?? undefined,
536	        strength,
537	        model_name: effectiveModel,
538	        params: taskParamsWithPlacement,
539	        based_on: basedOn ?? undefined,
540	        source_variant_id: sourceVariantId ?? undefined,
541	        generation_id: generationId ?? undefined,
542	        shot_id: shotId ?? undefined,
543	        ...(timelinePlacement ? { timeline_placement: timelinePlacement } : {}),
544	      };
545	
546	      const result = await createGenerationTask({
547	        ...generationArgs,
548	        idempotency_key: await buildBatchIdempotencyKey(generationArgs, index),
549	      });
550	
551	      if (result.result.startsWith("Failed to create task:")) {
552	        failedCount += 1;
553	      } else if (result.result.includes("(deduplicated)")) {
554	        deduplicatedCount += 1;
555	      } else {
556	        queuedCount += 1;
557	      }
558	    }
559	
560	    const summaryParts = [`Queued ${queuedCount} ${queuedCount === 1 ? "task" : "tasks"} with varied prompts.`];
561	    if (failedCount > 0) {
562	      summaryParts.push(`${failedCount} failed.`);
563	    }
564	    if (deduplicatedCount > 0) {
565	      summaryParts.push(`${deduplicatedCount} were duplicates of existing tasks.`);
566	    }
567	    if (requestedCount > MAX_BATCH_VARIATIONS) {
568	      summaryParts.push(`Requested ${requestedCount}, capped at ${MAX_BATCH_VARIATIONS}.`);
569	    }
570	
571	    return { result: `${summaryParts.join(" ")}${shotNote}`.trim() };
572	  }
573	
574	  const result = await createGenerationTask({
575	    project_id: timelineState.projectId,
576	    prompt: prompt ?? undefined,
577	    count: 1,
578	    task_type: taskType,
579	    reference_mode: referenceMode,
580	    reference_image_url: referenceImageUrls[0],
```

# Variation-Intent Feature: Implementation Plan

## Recommendations

**1. Where to derive variation intent.** Option (a) — timeline agent LLM fills it. Confirmed. The agent already has the full chat history, system prompt, and selected-clip context in its loop. Passing chat history to `ai-prompt` would double token cost, duplicate prompt engineering across two LLMs, and leak conversational state into a service that otherwise works on self-contained requests. Keep `ai-prompt` stateless; let the agent do the intent extraction it's already doing implicitly.

**2. Template redesign.** The current template fails because it simultaneously demands "distinct" and "keep descriptions as stated", and the `SIMPLE_SUBJECT_REMINDER` explicitly forbids embellishment — Kimi resolves this by duplicating. The fix is to relocate variance into an explicit, named axis (*who/which character* by default, or whatever the intent says) and to reframe "keep as stated" as applying to the **scene/core idea**, not the full prompt text. Proposed wording:

```
System: You are an image-prompt variation writer. Given one base prompt and
a variation axis, you output N prompts that describe the SAME core scene
expressed in N different ways along that axis. You never output duplicates.

User:
BASE PROMPT: <overallPromptText>
VARIATION AXIS: <variation_intent or default>
COUNT: N

DEFAULT AXIS (used when no axis is given):
"Same scene and action, but each prompt features a different person or
character (vary name, age, appearance, ethnicity, clothing). Everything else
— setting, mood, composition, style — stays the same as the base prompt."

RULES:
- The core subject matter, setting, and action of the base prompt MUST be
  preserved in every output. Do not invent new scenes.
- Vary ONLY along the stated axis. Do not add camera/lighting/style details
  unless the axis calls for them.
- Each of the N outputs must be textually distinct from the others AND from
  the base prompt. If you find yourself repeating, push harder on the axis.
- Output N lines. No numbering, no quotes, no commentary.

EXAMPLES (axis = default, base = "a person reading on a park bench", N=3):
A young woman named Lin with short black hair reads a paperback on a park bench
An elderly man with a grey beard named Walter reads a newspaper on a park bench
A teenage boy in a red hoodie named Diego reads a comic on a park bench
```

Why Kimi will respect this: the conflict is removed. "Keep as stated" becomes "preserve the scene"; variation is named explicitly as an axis with a required dimension to differ on. The default axis gives Kimi a concrete instruction ("different person") rather than the abstract "distinct", which is what was causing the collapse to verbatim copies. The example set demonstrates the exact output distribution we want.

**3. Model switch.** Keep Kimi for now with the new template; revisit only if eval shows continued echoing. Kimi K2 on Groq is ~5-10x faster and cheaper than Haiku 4.5 via Anthropic, and this is an interactive blocking path — template fix should solve it without paying the latency tax. Add a `llama-3.3-70b-versatile` fallback trigger on duplicate-detection (see step 4 below).

**4. Other callers.** Only one: `src/shared/hooks/ai/useAIInteractionService.ts:94` (the in-app prompt-list generator used by non-agent UI). Adding an optional `variationIntent` field is backward-compatible — omit it and the default axis kicks in. Verify `ai-prompt/index.test.ts` still passes with the new optional field.

**5. Schema shape.** Freeform string, max ~200 chars, described in the tool schema with examples. A structured enum (`character | lighting | camera | style | pose | time_of_day`) is tempting but would force the agent to map user intent onto a fixed taxonomy and lose nuance ("different dog breeds", "different decades"). Freeform lets the agent write `"vary the dog breed, keep everything else identical"` which flows directly into the template. Cost: the template must tolerate fuzzy axes — that's a prompt-engineering problem the model already handles well.

**6. Fallback behavior.** Replace `numberedFallback` with a two-step strategy: (a) if `ai-prompt` returns fewer than N unique prompts, **retry once server-side in `ai-prompt` with the llama fallback model and temperature bumped to 1.0**; (b) if still short, return the unique set only with a `partial: true` flag, and have `executeCreateTask` queue only those tasks and surface `"Queued K of N (K unique variations available)"` to the user. Never pad with `(variation N)` suffixes — the GPU model treats them as noise tokens and generates near-duplicate images, which is the exact failure mode we're fixing. Failing loud (partial batch + honest count) is better than producing slop.

## Step-by-Step Plan

1. **`supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tool-schemas.ts`** (~line 143 neighborhood): add to `create_task` properties:
   ```
   variation_intent: {
     type: "string",
     description: "Optional axis of variation when count > 1. Describe in plain English what should differ between outputs, e.g. 'different characters', 'different lighting and time of day', 'different camera angles of the same subject'. Omit to default to 'same scene, different characters/people'. Derived from the user's chat request."
   }
   ```

2. **`supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts`**: in the system prompt section that documents `create_task`, add a sentence telling the LLM to fill `variation_intent` from user phrasing ("make 10 with different lighting" → `"different lighting conditions"`) and to leave it blank if the user didn't specify.

3. **`tools/create-task.ts`**:
   - `expandPrompts(basePrompt, count, logger, variationIntent?)` — new optional param.
   - Thread `variation_intent` from `args.variation_intent` (via `asTrimmedString`) into the call at line 519.
   - POST body gets `variationIntent` field.
   - Replace `numberedFallback` callers: when `ai-prompt` returns partial, return the unique subset (not padded); `executeCreateTask` then queues `Math.min(expandedPrompts.length, targetCount)` tasks and reports the actual count.
   - Remove the `while (prompts.length < count) prompts.push(basePrompt)` padding at line 224.
   - Keep `ensureDistinctPrompts` but change its fallback branch to either return short or delegate to caller — not emit `(variation N)` strings.

4. **`supabase/functions/ai-prompt/index.ts`** `generate_prompts` case: read `variationIntent` from body, pass into `buildGeneratePromptsMessages`. On duplicate detection (normalize + dedupe; < N unique), retry once with `GROQ_FALLBACK_MODEL` and `temperature: 1.0`. Log both paths.

5. **`supabase/functions/ai-prompt/templates.ts`**: rewrite `buildGeneratePromptsMessages` as above. Drop `SIMPLE_SUBJECT_REMINDER` from this template (it's the root of the echoing conflict). New signature takes `variationIntent?: string`.

6. **`supabase/functions/ai-prompt/templates.test.ts` / `index.test.ts`**: add cases for (a) default axis produces distinct character variations, (b) explicit axis is obeyed, (c) partial response triggers fallback model path.

7. **`src/shared/hooks/ai/useAIInteractionService.ts:85`**: extend `GeneratePromptsParams` with optional `variationIntent`, forward in the POST body. No behavior change when absent.

## Risks / Open Questions

- **Existing gallery-side callers** (`useAIInteractionService`) may currently *rely* on the "keep as stated, don't embellish" behavior for simple prompt lists — the removal of `SIMPLE_SUBJECT_REMINDER` could cause regression there. Verify with the user whether the in-app "generate more prompts" UI should also adopt the axis-based default, or whether that path needs a separate template branch (e.g., `variationIntent: "literal"` means old behavior).
- **Kimi behavior on short axes**: if `variation_intent` is one word ("characters"), Kimi may still under-vary. Consider having the agent LLM write full-sentence intents ("vary the character's age, ethnicity, and clothing"), enforced via the tool-schema description examples.
- **Idempotency keys** are hashed over prompt text (line 306). Partial batches producing fewer unique prompts means fewer tasks — confirm this is what we want vs. failing the whole batch.
- **MAX_BATCH_VARIATIONS = 16** cap applies before intent-aware expansion; unchanged, but worth confirming with the user that 16 remains the ceiling.
- **No test fixture for Kimi's actual output** — the template fix is hypothesis-driven. Recommend a manual eval (10 base prompts × 3 axes) before shipping.

> DEVELOPER

Implement a variation-intent feature across the Reigh ai-prompt and ai-timeline-agent edge functions. This is a coding task — write and edit files, don't just plan.

## Background

When users chat with the timeline agent and ask "make 10 more", the agent's `create_task` tool (count > 1) calls `expandPrompts` → the `ai-prompt` edge function's `generate_prompts` case, which uses Kimi K2 via Groq to produce N varied prompts. The current template has internal conflicts ("Generate N distinct prompts" vs "keep user's descriptions as stated" vs `SIMPLE_SUBJECT_REMINDER` which tells the model not to embellish), and Kimi resolves this by returning N verbatim copies of the user's base prompt. Then `ensureDistinctPrompts` dedups to 1 and pads with `numberedFallback` → prompts like `"{base} (variation 17)"` — useless noise tokens that produce near-identical images.

I debugged this with a logger threaded through `expandPrompts`; the `uniqueNormalized: 1` metadata confirmed Kimi is echoing. That instrumentation should stay (it's valuable ongoing visibility), but the fallback behavior should change.

## Design decisions (already made, don't redesign)

1. **Variation intent lives on the timeline agent tool.** Add an optional `variation_intent` string arg to the `create_task` tool schema. The agent LLM fills it by reading the user's chat request — no structured enum, freeform English.

2. **Default behavior (no intent given) is LINGUISTIC REWRITES ONLY.** N prompts expressing the *exact same concept* — same subject, same scene, same mood, same characters, same everything — with different sentence structures, synonyms, clause ordering, phrasing. Think "paraphrase N ways", not "variation". The 10 outputs should feel like 10 rewrites of one prompt, not 10 different prompts.

3. **When intent is given, follow it literally.** "different scenes" → vary scenes. "different characters" → vary characters. "different lighting" → vary lighting. The intent overrides the linguistic-rewrite default.

4. **Model stays Kimi K2.** The bug is the template, not the model. (But add a duplicate-detection retry — see step 6 below.)

5. **Kill `(variation N)` padding.** `numberedFallback` is harmful for image generation — the suffix is noise to the GPU model. Replace with: return the unique subset only; `executeCreateTask` queues that shorter batch and tells the user honestly ("Queued 3 of 10 — only 3 distinct variations available").

6. **Server-side retry in ai-prompt.** If Groq returns fewer than N unique prompts after normalization, retry once with `llama-3.3-70b-versatile` and `temperature: 1.0`. Log both paths.

## New template for `buildGeneratePromptsMessages`

Write it following this spec. You can adjust wording, but these constraints are non-negotiable:

- **No contradictory instructions.** The current template fails because "distinct" and "keep as stated" fight each other. The new template names what must stay fixed AND what must vary, with no ambiguity.
- **Default axis is explicit**: when no `variationIntent` is supplied, the instruction must say "express the same concept in N different ways — vary sentence structure, synonyms, clause ordering, phrasing. Keep subject/scene/mood/characters/details identical."
- **Intent-mode axis**: when `variationIntent` is supplied, the instruction must say "vary along this axis: {variationIntent}. Keep everything else from the base prompt fixed."
- **Drop `SIMPLE_SUBJECT_REMINDER`** from this template — it's the root cause.
- **Keep the format rules** (one prompt per line, no numbering, no quotes).
- **Include 2-3 example outputs for each mode** (default and intent) — these carry the actual behavior. Pick small, clear base prompts.
- Kimi K2 is the target model; prefer direct imperative phrasing over soft "try to" language.

- Update `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.test.ts` — existing tests may break from `ensureDistinctPrompts` behavior change. Add case: when ai-prompt returns only K<N unique prompts, `executeCreateTask` queues K tasks and returns a summary that reports K, not N.

```
110	export function buildEnhanceSegmentUserPrompt(prompt: string): string {
111	  return `You are viewing two images side by side: the LEFT image (with GREEN border) shows the STARTING frame, and the RIGHT image (with RED border) shows the ENDING frame of a video sequence.
112	
113	The user's input is: '${prompt}'
114	
115	CRITICAL - DETERMINE THE USER'S INTENT:
116	1. If the user's input already reads like a DIRECT PROMPT (describes visuals, camera movement, scene elements in complete sentences), PRESERVE THEIR EXACT WORDING as much as possible. Only expand to three sentences if needed, using their original phrasing as the foundation.
117	2. If the user's input is INSTRUCTIONS (e.g., "describe...", "make it more...", "add...") or a brief description that needs elaboration, then create a detailed three-sentence prompt.
118	
119	EXAMPLES OF DIRECT PROMPTS (preserve these):
120	- "the camera flies through the sky to the distant hills as the snow storm begins" -> This IS the prompt. Keep this wording.
121	- "A woman walks through the garden as petals fall around her" -> This IS the prompt. Keep this wording.
122	
123	EXAMPLES OF INSTRUCTIONS (elaborate these):
124	- "describe the camera moving through clouds" -> User wants you to write the prompt
125	- "something dramatic with a storm" -> User wants you to create details
126	- "make it cinematic" -> User wants elaboration
127	
128	FOCUS ON MOTION: Describe what MOVES, what CHANGES, and HOW things transition between these frames. Everything should be described in terms of motion and transformation, not static states.
129	
130	YOUR RESPONSE MUST FOLLOW THIS EXACT STRUCTURE:
131	
132	SENTENCE 1 (PRIMARY MOTION): Describe the main action, camera movement, and major scene transitions. What is the dominant movement happening?
133	
134	SENTENCE 2 (MOVING ELEMENTS): Describe how the characters, objects, and environment are moving or changing. Focus on what's in motion and how it moves through space.
135	
136	SENTENCE 3 (MOTION DETAILS): Describe the subtle motion details - secondary movements, environmental dynamics, particles, lighting shifts, and small-scale motions.
137	
138	Examples of MOTION-FOCUSED descriptions:
139	
140	- "The sun rises rapidly above the jagged peaks as the camera tilts upward from the dark valley floor. The silhouette pine trees sway gently against the shifting violet and gold sky as the entire landscape brightens. Wisps of morning mist evaporate and drift upward from the river surface while distant birds circle and glide through the upper left corner."
141	
142	- "A woman sprints from the kitchen into the bright exterior sunlight as the camera pans right to track her accelerating path. Her vintage floral dress flows and ripples in the wind while colorful playground equipment blurs past in the background. 144	- "The camera zooms aggressively inward into a macro shot of an eye as the brown horse reflection grows larger and more detailed. The iris textures shift under the changing warm lighting while the biological details come into sharper focus. The pupil constricts and contracts in reaction to the light while the tiny reflected horse tosses its mane and shifts position."

146	Now create your THREE-SENTENCE MOTION-FOCUSED description based on: '${prompt}'
```

```
18	const GROQ_MODEL = "moonshotai/kimi-k2-instruct-0905";
19	const GROQ_FALLBACK_MODEL = "llama-3.3-70b-versatile";
20	const GROQ_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;

22	let groqClient: Groq | null = null;
23	
24	function getGroqClient(): Groq {
25	  if (groqClient) {
26	    return groqClient;
27	  }
28	  const apiKey = Deno.env.get("GROQ_API_KEY");
29	  if (!apiKey) {
30	    throw new Error("[ai-prompt] Missing Groq provider configuration");
31	  }
32	  groqClient = new Groq({ apiKey, timeout: GROQ_TIMEOUT_MS });
33	  return groqClient;
34	}
35	
36	interface GroqChatParams {
37	  model: string;
38	  messages: { role: string; content: string }[];
39	  temperature: number;
40	  max_tokens: number;
41	  top_p?: number;
42	}
43	
44	async function groqWithFallback(
45	  groq: Groq,
46	  params: GroqChatParams,
47	  logger: { info: (msg: string) => void },
48	) {
49	  try {
50	    return await groq.chat.completions.create(params as Parameters<typeof groq.chat.completions.create>[0]);
51	  } catch (err: unknown) {
52	    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
53	    const isModelError = msg.includes("model") || msg.includes("timeout") || msg.includes("abort") || msg.includes("could not process");
54	    if (isModelError && params.model !== GROQ_FALLBACK_MODEL) {
55	      logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] Primary model failed (${msg}), falling back to ${GROQ_FALLBACK_MODEL}`);
56	      return await groq.chat.completions.create({
57	        ...params,
58	        model: GROQ_FALLBACK_MODEL,
59	      } as Parameters<typeof groq.chat.completions.create>[0]);
60	    }
61	    throw err;
62	  }
63	}
64	
65	function getOpenAIApiKey(): string {
66	  const apiKey = Deno.env.get("OPENAI_API_KEY");
67	  if (!apiKey) {
68	    throw new Error("[ai-prompt] Missing OpenAI provider configuration");
69	  }
70	  return apiKey;
71	}
72	
73	serve(async (req) => {
74	  const bootstrap = await bootstrapEdgeHandler(req, {

76	    logPrefix: "[AI-PROMPT]",
77	    parseBody: "strict",
78	    auth: {
79	      required: true,
80	      options: { allowJwtUserAuth: true },
81	    },
82	    ...NO_SESSION_RUNTIME_OPTIONS,
83	  });
84	  if (!bootstrap.ok) {
85	    return bootstrap.response;
86	  }
87	
88	  const { supabaseAdmin, logger, auth, body } = bootstrap.value;
89	  if (!auth?.userId && !auth?.isServiceRole) {
90	    return jsonResponse({ error: "Authentication failed" }, 401);
91	  }
92	
93	  // Rate limit by user ID (skip for service role — internal agent calls)
94	  if (auth.userId) {
95	    const rateLimitDenied = await enforceRateLimit({
96	      supabaseAdmin,
97	      functionName: 'ai-prompt',
98	      userId: auth.userId,
99	      config: RATE_LIMITS.expensive,
100	      logger,
101	      logPrefix: '[AI-PROMPT]',
102	      responses: {
103	        serviceUnavailable: () => jsonResponse({ error: 'Rate limit service unavailable' }, 503),
104	      },
105	    });
106	    if (rateLimitDenied) {
107	      return rateLimitDenied;
108	    }
109	  }
110	
111	  const task = body.task as string | undefined;
112	  if (!task) return jsonResponse({ error: "task is required" }, 400);
113	
114	  try {
115	    switch (task) {
116	      case "generate_prompts": {
117	        const groq = getGroqClient();
118	        const overallPromptText = String(body.overallPromptText ?? "");
119	        const rulesToRememberText = String(body.rulesToRememberText ?? "");
120	        const numberToGenerate = Number(body.numberToGenerate ?? 3);
121	        const existingPrompts = Array.isArray(body.existingPrompts) ? body.existingPrompts as unknown[] : [];
122	        const temperature = Number(body.temperature ?? 0.8);
123	
124	        const { systemMsg, userMsg } = buildGeneratePromptsMessages({
125	          overallPromptText,
126	          rulesToRememberText,
127	          numberToGenerate,
128	          existingPrompts,
129	        });
130	
131	        logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_prompts: starting Groq call, model=${GROQ_MODEL}, numberToGenerate=${numberToGenerate}`);
132	        const groqStart = Date.now();
133	        const resp = await groqWithFallback(groq, {
134	          model: GROQ_MODEL,
135	          messages: [
136	            { role: "system", content: systemMsg },
137	            { role: "user", content: userMsg },
138	          ],
139	          temperature: temperature,
140	          max_tokens: 4096,
141	          top_p: 1,
142	        }, logger);
143	        logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_prompts: Groq call completed in ${Date.now() - groqStart}ms, model=${resp.model}`);
144	        const outputText = resp.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || "";
145	        const prompts = outputText.split("\n").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
146	        
147	        // Validate we got the expected number of prompts
148	        if (prompts.length !== numberToGenerate) {
149	          // If we got too many, take the first N
150	          if (prompts.length > numberToGenerate) {
151	            prompts.splice(numberToGenerate);
152	          }
153	          // If we got too few, we'll just return what we have rather than failing
154	        }
155	        
156	        return jsonResponse({ prompts, usage: resp.usage });
157	      }
158	      case "edit_prompt": {
159	        const groq = getGroqClient();
160	        const originalPromptText = String(body.originalPromptText ?? "");
161	        const editInstructions = String(body.editInstructions ?? "");
162	        if (!originalPromptText || !editInstructions) return jsonResponse({ error: "originalPromptText and editInstructions required" }, 400);
163	        const { systemMsg, userMsg } = buildEditPromptMessages({
164	          originalPromptText,
165	          editInstructions,
166	        });
167	        const resp = await groqWithFallback(groq, {
168	          model: GROQ_MODEL,
169	          messages: [
170	            { role: "system", content: systemMsg },
171	            { role: "user", content: userMsg },
172	          ],
173	          temperature: 0.7,
174	          max_tokens: 2048,
175	          top_p: 1,
176	        }, logger);
177	        const newText = resp.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || originalPromptText;
178	        return jsonResponse({ success: true, newText, usage: resp.usage });
179	      }
180	      case "generate_summary": {
181	        const groq = getGroqClient();
182	        const promptText = String(body.promptText ?? "");
183	        if (!promptText) return jsonResponse({ error: "promptText required" }, 400);
184	        logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_summary: starting Groq call`);
185	        const summaryStart = Date.now();
186	        const resp = await groqWithFallback(groq, {
187	          model: GROQ_MODEL,
188	          messages: [{ role: "user", content: `Create a brief summary of this image prompt in 10 words or less. Output only the summary text with no additional formatting or quotation marks:
189	
190	"${promptText}"
191	
192	Summary:` }],
193	          temperature: 1.0,
194	          max_tokens: 50,
195	          top_p: 1,
196	        }, logger);
197	        logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_summary: Groq call completed in ${Date.now() - summaryStart}ms`);
198	        const summary = resp.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || null;
199	        return jsonResponse({ summary, usage: resp.usage });
200	      }
201	      case "enhance_segment_prompt": {
202	        // Enhance segment prompt(s) using OpenAI GPT-5 Mini
203	        // Accepts either a single `prompt` string or an array `prompts` for batch processing.
204	        const isBatch = Array.isArray(body.prompts);
205	        const prompts: string[] = isBatch
206	          ? (body.prompts as string[]).map((p: unknown) => String(p ?? ""))
207	          : [String(body.prompt ?? "")];
208	
209	        if (!isBatch && !prompts[0].trim()) {
210	          return jsonResponse({ error: "prompt is required" }, 400);
211	        }
212	
213	        const openaiApiKey = getOpenAIApiKey();
214	        const systemMsg = ENHANCE_SEGMENT_SYSTEM_PROMPT;
215	
216	        const MAX_RETRIES = 2;
217	        const RETRY_DELAY_MS = 2000;
218	
219	        async function callOpenAI(p: string): Promise<Response> {
220	          return fetch("https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions", {
221	            method: "POST",
222	            headers: {
223	              "Authorization": `Bearer ${openaiApiKey}`,
224	              "Content-Type": "application/json",
225	            },
226	            body: JSON.stringify({
227	              model: "gpt-5-mini",
228	              messages: [
229	                { role: "system", content: systemMsg },
230	                { role: "user", content: buildEnhanceSegmentUserPrompt(p) },
231	              ],
232	              max_completion_tokens: 16000,
233	            }),
234	          });
235	        }
236	
237	        function delay(ms: number): Promise<void> {
238	          return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
239	        }
240	
241	        async function enhanceOne(p: string): Promise<{ enhanced_prompt: string; usage?: unknown }> {
242	          if (!p.trim()) return { enhanced_prompt: "" };
243	
244	          for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= MAX_RETRIES; attempt++) {
245	            try {
246	              const response = await callOpenAI(p);
247	
248	              if (response.status === 429) {
249	                console.warn(`[ai-prompt] OpenAI 429, attempt ${attempt + 1}/${MAX_RETRIES + 1}`);
250	                if (attempt < MAX_RETRIES) {
251	                  await delay(RETRY_DELAY_MS * (attempt + 1));
252	                  continue;
253	                }
254	                return { enhanced_prompt: p };
255	              }
256	
257	              if (!response.ok) {
258	                const errorText = await response.text();
259	                console.error(`[ai-prompt] OpenAI API error:`, response.status, errorText);
260	                return { enhanced_prompt: p };
261	              }
262	
263	              const data = await response.json();
264	              return {
265	                enhanced_prompt: data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || p,
266	                usage: data.usage,
267	              };
268	            } catch (err) {
269	              console.error(`[ai-prompt] OpenAI fetch error attempt ${attempt + 1}:`, toErrorMessage(err));
270	              if (attempt < MAX_RETRIES) {
271	                await delay(RETRY_DELAY_MS * (attempt + 1));
272	                continue;
273	              }
274	              return { enhanced_prompt: p };
275	            }
276	          }
277	
278	          return { enhanced_prompt: p };
279	        }
280	
281	        try {
282	          if (!isBatch) {
283	            const result = await enhanceOne(prompts[0]);
284	            return jsonResponse(result);
285	          }
286	
287	          // Batch: fire all concurrently server-side
288	          const results = await Promise.all(prompts.map(enhanceOne));
289	          return jsonResponse({
290	            enhanced_prompts: results.map(r => r.enhanced_prompt),
291	            usage: results.map(r => r.usage).filter(Boolean),
292	          });
293	        } catch (fetchError: unknown) {
294	          const message = toErrorMessage(fetchError);
295	          console.error(`[ai-prompt] OpenAI fetch error:`, message);
296	          return jsonResponse({ error: "Failed to call OpenAI API", details: message }, 500);
297	        }
298	      }
299	      default:
300	        return jsonResponse({ error: `Unknown task: ${task}` }, 400);
301	    }
302	  } catch (err: unknown) {
303	    const message = toErrorMessage(err);
304	    console.error(`[ai-prompt] Error handling task ${task}:`, message);
305	    return jsonResponse({ error: "Internal server error", details: message }, 500);
306	  }
```

```
23	const SUPPORTED_CREATE_TASK_TYPES = new Set([
24	  "text-to-image",
25	  "style-transfer",
26	  "subject-transfer",
27	  "style-character-transfer",
28	  "scene-transfer",
29	  "image-to-video",
30	  "image-to-image",
31	  "magic-edit",
32	  "image-upscale",
33	  "video-enhance",
34	  "character-animate",
35	]);
36	const TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE: Record<string, string> = {
37	  "style-transfer": "style",
38	  "subject-transfer": "subject",
39	  "style-character-transfer": "style-character",
40	  "scene-transfer": "scene",
41	};
42	const TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_PROMPT = new Set([
43	  "text-to-image",
44	  "style-transfer",
45	  "subject-transfer",
46	  "style-character-transfer",
47	  "scene-transfer",
48	  "image-to-video",
49	  "image-to-image",
50	  "magic-edit",
51	]);
52	const TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_REFERENCE_IMAGE = new Set([
53	  "style-transfer",
54	  "subject-transfer",
55	  "style-character-transfer",
56	  "scene-transfer",
57	  "image-to-image",
58	  "magic-edit",
59	  "image-upscale",
60	  "character-animate",
61	]);
62	const TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_VIDEO = new Set([
63	  "video-enhance",
64	  "character-animate",
65	]);
66	const TASK_TYPES_SUPPORTING_SOURCE_PLACEMENT = new Set([
67	  "image-to-image",
68	  "magic-edit",
69	  "image-upscale",
70	]);
71	
72	function asFiniteNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
73	  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
74	}
75	
76	function normalizeTimelinePlacementArg(value: unknown): TimelinePlacement | undefined {
77	  if (!isRecord(value)) {
78	    return undefined;
79	  }
80	
81	  const timelineId = asTrimmedString(value.timeline_id);
82	  const sourceClipId = asTrimmedString(value.source_clip_id);
83	  const targetTrack = asTrimmedString(value.target_track);
84	  const insertionTime = typeof value.insertion_time === "number" && Number.isFinite(value.insertion_time)
85	    ? value.insertion_time
86	    : undefined;
87	  const intent = value.intent === "after_source" || value.intent === "replace"
88	    ? value.intent
89	    : undefined;
90	
91	  if (!timelineId || !sourceClipId || !targetTrack || insertionTime === undefined || !intent) {
92	    return undefined;
93	  }
94	
95	  return {
96	    timeline_id: timelineId,
97	    source_clip_id: sourceClipId,
98	    target_track: targetTrack,
99	    insertion_time: insertionTime,
100	    intent,
101	  };
102	}
103	
104	function buildPlacementIntent(
105	  taskType: string,
106	  resolvedSelectionContexts: ReturnType<typeof resolveSelectionContext>,
107	): PlacementIntent | undefined {
108	  if (!TASK_TYPES_SUPPORTING_SOURCE_PLACEMENT.has(taskType)) {
109	    return undefined;
110	  }
111	
112	  const timelineSelections = resolvedSelectionContexts.filter((context) => context.is_on_timeline);
113	  if (timelineSelections.length !== 1) {
114	    return undefined;
115	  }
116	
117	  const anchor = timelineSelections[0];
118	  return {
119	    timeline_id: anchor.timeline_id,
120	    anchor_clip_id: anchor.clip_id,
121	    ...(anchor.generation_id ? { anchor_generation_id: anchor.generation_id } : {}),
122	    ...(anchor.variant_id ? { anchor_variant_id: anchor.variant_id } : {}),
123	    relation: "after",
124	    preferred_track_id: anchor.track_id,
125	    fallback_at: anchor.at + anchor.duration,
126	    fallback_track_id: anchor.track_id,
127	  };
128	}
129	
130	function withPlacementIntent(
131	  params: Record<string, unknown> | undefined,
132	  placementIntent: PlacementIntent | undefined,
133	): Record<string, unknown> | undefined {
134	  if (!placementIntent) {
135	    return params;
136	  }
137	
138	  return {
139	    ...(params ?? {}),
140	    placement_intent: placementIntent,
141	  };
142	}
143	
144	// Default reference strength params per mode — matches the form's REFERENCE_MODE_DEFAULTS for qwen-image
145	const REFERENCE_MODE_DEFAULTS: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>> = {
146	  style: { style_reference_strength: 1.1 },
147	  subject: { style_reference_strength: 0.4, subject_strength: 1.0 },
148	  "style-character": { style_reference_strength: 0.4, subject_strength: 1.0 },
149	  scene: { style_reference_strength: 0.4, in_this_scene: true, in_this_scene_strength: 1.0 },
150	};
151	
152	async function expandPrompts(
153	  basePrompt: string,
154	  count: number,
155	  logger?: ExpandPromptsLogger,
156	): Promise<string[]> {
157	  const logError = (message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) => {
158	    if (logger?.error) logger.error(message, context);
159	    else console.error(message, context ?? "");
160	  };
161	  const logWarn = (message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) => {
162	    if (logger?.warn) logger.warn(message, context);
163	    else console.warn(message, context ?? "");
164	  };
165	  if (logger?.info) {
166	    logger.info("[agent] expandPrompts: called", { count, loggerPresent: true });
167	  } else {
168	    console.log("[agent] expandPrompts: called (no logger)", { count });
169	  }
170	
171	  try {
172	    const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL");
173	    const serviceRoleKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY");
174	    if (!supabaseUrl || !serviceRoleKey) {
175	      logError("[agent] expandPrompts: missing SUPABASE_URL or SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, using fallback");
176	      return numberedFallback(basePrompt, count);
177	    }
178	
179	    const response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/ai-prompt`, {
180	      method: "POST",
181	      headers: {
182	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
183	        Authorization: `Bearer ${serviceRoleKey}`,
184	        apikey: serviceRoleKey,
185	      },
186	      body: JSON.stringify({
187	        task: "generate_prompts",
188	        overallPromptText: basePrompt,
189	        numberToGenerate: count,
190	        existingPrompts: [],
191	        temperature: 0.9,
192	      }),
193	    });
194	    if (!response.ok) {
195	      const body = await response.text().catch(() => "<unreadable>");
196	      logError("[agent] expandPrompts: ai-prompt HTTP error, using fallback", {
197	        status: response.status,
198	        statusText: response.statusText,
199	        body: body.slice(0, 500),
200	        count,
201	      });
202	      return numberedFallback(basePrompt, count);
203	    }
204	    const data = await response.json() as { prompts?: string[] };
205	    if (Array.isArray(data.prompts) && data.prompts.length > 0) {
206	      const prompts = data.prompts.slice(0, count);
207	      const uniqueNormalized = new Set(
208	        prompts.map((p) => p.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().toLowerCase()),
209	      );
210	      if (logger?.info) {
211	        logger.info("[agent] expandPrompts: ai-prompt returned", {
212	          requested: count,
213	          returned: data.prompts.length,
214	          uniqueNormalized: uniqueNormalized.size,
215	          firstTwoHeads: prompts.slice(0, 2).map((p) => p.slice(0, 120)),
216	        });
217	      }
218	      if (prompts.length < count) {
219	        logWarn("[agent] expandPrompts: ai-prompt returned partial result, padding with basePrompt", {
220	          returned: prompts.length,
221	          requested: count,
222	        });
223	      }
224	      while (prompts.length < count) prompts.push(basePrompt);
225	      return prompts;
226	    }
227	    logError("[agent] expandPrompts: ai-prompt returned no prompts, using fallback", {
228	      responseKeys: Object.keys(data ?? {}),
229	      count,
230	    });
231	  } catch (err) {
232	    const message = err instanceof Error ? `${err.name}: ${err.message}` : String(err);
233	    logError("[agent] expandPrompts: threw, using fallback", { error: message, count });
234	  }
235	  return numberedFallback(basePrompt, count);
236	}
237	
238	function numberedFallback(basePrompt: string, count: number): string[] {
239	  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) =>
240	    i === 0 ? basePrompt : `${basePrompt} (variation ${i + 1})`
241	  );
242	}
243	
244	function normalizePromptKey(prompt: string): string {
245	  return prompt.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().toLowerCase();
246	}
247	
248	function ensureDistinctPrompts(basePrompt: string, prompts: string[], count: number): string[] {
249	  const uniquePrompts: string[] = [];
250	  const seen = new Set<string>();
251	
252	  for (const candidate of prompts) {
253	    const prompt = candidate.trim();
254	    if (!prompt) {
255	      continue;
256	    }
257	
258	    const [REDACTED](prompt);
259	    if (seen.has(key)) {
260	      continue;
261	    }
262	
263	    seen.add(key);
264	    uniquePrompts.push(prompt);
265	
266	    if (uniquePrompts.length >= count) {
267	      return uniquePrompts;
268	    }
269	  }
270	
271	  let fallbackIndex = 1;
272	  while (uniquePrompts.length < count) {
273	    const fallbackPrompt = numberedFallback(basePrompt, count + fallbackIndex)[count + fallbackIndex - 1];
274	    fallbackIndex += 1;
275	
276	    const [REDACTED](fallbackPrompt);
277	    if (seen.has(key)) {
278	      continue;
279	    }
280	
281	    seen.add(key);
282	    uniquePrompts.push(fallbackPrompt);
283	  }
284	
285	  return uniquePrompts;
286	}
287	
288	async function sha256Hex(value: string): Promise<string> {
289	  const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(value);
290	  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", encoded);
291	  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest))
292	    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
293	    .join("");
294	}
295	
296	// Batch timestamp ensures "10 more" creates new tasks instead of deduplicating against previous batches
297	let batchTimestamp: string | null = null;
298	function getBatchTimestamp(): string {
299	  if (!batchTimestamp) {
300	    batchTimestamp = Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);
301	  }
302	  return batchTimestamp;
303	}
304	
305	async function buildBatchIdempotencyKey(args: CreateGenerationTaskArgs, batchIndex: number): Promise<string> {
306	  const payload = JSON.stringify({
307	    project_id: args.project_id,
308	    request: {
309	      prompt: args.prompt,
310	      task_type: args.task_type,
311	      reference_mode: args.reference_mode,
312	      reference_image_url: args.reference_image_url,
313	      shot_id: args.shot_id,
314	      model_name: args.model_name,
315	      image_urls: args.image_urls,
316	      video_url: args.video_url,
317	      strength: args.strength,
318	      based_on: args.based_on,
319	      generation_id: args.generation_id,
320	      timeline_placement: args.timeline_placement,
321	      params: args.params,
322	      batch_index: batchIndex,
323	      batch_ts: getBatchTimestamp(),
324	    },
325	  });
326	  const digest = await sha256Hex(payload);
327	  return `timeline-agent:${digest.slice(0, 40)}`;
328	}
329	
330	export async function executeCreateTask(
331	  args: Record<string, unknown>,
332	  timelineState: TimelineState,
333	  selectedClips: SelectedClipPayload[] | undefined,
334	  supabaseAdmin: SupabaseAdmin,
335	  generationContext?: GenerationContext,
336	  timelineId = "",
337	  logger?: ExpandPromptsLogger,
338	): Promise<Pick<ToolResult, "result">> {
339	  const imageContext = generationContext?.image ?? null;
340	  const travelContext = generationContext?.travel ?? null;
341	  const taskType = asTrimmedString(args.task_type);
342	  const prompt = asTrimmedString(args.prompt);
343	  if (!taskType) {
344	    return { result: "create_task requires task_type." };
345	  }
346	  if (!SUPPORTED_CREATE_TASK_TYPES.has(taskType)) {
347	    return { result: `create_task does not support task_type ${taskType}.` };
348	  }
349	  if (TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_PROMPT.has(taskType) && !prompt) {
350	    return { result: `create_task ${taskType} requires prompt.` };
351	  }
352	
353	  const requestedReferenceImageUrls = asStringArray(args.reference_image_urls);
354	  const activeReference = imageContext?.activeReference ?? null;
355	  const shouldUseActiveReference = requestedReferenceImageUrls.length === 0
356	    && Boolean(TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE[taskType])
357	    && Boolean(activeReference?.url);
358	  const referenceImageUrls = shouldUseActiveReference && activeReference?.url
359	    ? [activeReference.url]
360	    : requestedReferenceImageUrls;
361	  const referenceMode = asTrimmedString(args.reference_mode) ?? TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE[taskType] ?? undefined;
362	  const videoUrl = asTrimmedString(args.video_url);
363	  const strength = typeof args.strength === "number" && Number.isFinite(args.strength) ? args.strength : undefined;
364	  if (strength !== undefined && (strength < 0 || strength > 1)) {
365	    return { result: "create_task strength must be between 0 and 1." };
366	  }
367	  const timelinePlacement = normalizeTimelinePlacementArg(args.timeline_placement);
368	  if (args.timeline_placement !== undefined && !timelinePlacement) {
369	    return {
370	      result: "create_task timeline_placement must include timeline_id, source_clip_id, target_track, insertion_time, and intent.",
371	    };
372	  }
373	  if (taskType === "image-to-video" && referenceImageUrls.length < 1) {
374	    return { result: "create_task image-to-video requires at least one reference_image_url." };
375	  }
376	  if (TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_REFERENCE_IMAGE.has(taskType) && referenceImageUrls.length === 0) {
377	    return { result: `create_task ${taskType} requires reference_image_urls.` };
378	  }
379	  if (TASK_TYPES_REQUIRING_VIDEO.has(taskType) && !videoUrl) {
380	    return { result: `create_task ${taskType} requires video_url.` };
381	  }
382	
383	  const resolvedSelectionContexts = resolveSelectionContext(selectedClips ?? [], timelineState, timelineId);
384	  const selectedClipEntries = (selectedClips ?? []).map((clip, index) => ({
385	    clip,
386	    resolvedContext: resolvedSelectionContexts[index],
387	  }));
388	  const selectedReferenceEntries = selectedClipEntries.filter(({ clip }) => referenceImageUrls.includes(clip.url));
389	  const selectedVideoEntry = videoUrl
390	    ? selectedClipEntries.find(({ clip }) => clip.url === videoUrl)
391	    : undefined;
392	  const selectedEntriesForShot = selectedVideoEntry && !selectedReferenceEntries.some(({ clip }) => clip.url === selectedVideoEntry.clip.url)
393	    ? [...selectedReferenceEntries, selectedVideoEntry]
394	    : selectedReferenceEntries;
395	  const selectedClipsForShot = selectedEntriesForShot.map(({ clip }) => clip);
396	  const placementIntent = buildPlacementIntent(taskType, resolvedSelectionContexts);
397	  const generationIds = resolveClipGenerationIds(selectedClipsForShot, timelineState.registry, timelineState.config);
398	  let shotId = (
399	    await resolveSelectedClipShot(supabaseAdmin, timelineState, selectedClipsForShot)
400	  ).shotId;
401	  let shotNote = "";
402	  const shotName = asTrimmedString(args.shot_name);
403	  const needsAutoShot = !shotId && generationIds.length > 0 && taskType === "image-to-video";
404	  if (!shotId && (shotName || needsAutoShot) && generationIds.length > 0) {
405	    const effectiveShotName = shotName || `Shot ${new Date().toISOString().slice(11, 19)}`;
406	    shotId = await createShotWithGenerations(supabaseAdmin, {
407	      projectId: timelineState.projectId,
408	      shotName: effectiveShotName,
409	      generationIds,
410	      position: APPEND_SHOT_POSITION,
411	    });
412	    shotNote = ` Created shot ${effectiveShotName} (${shotId}).`;
413	  } else if (shotId) {
414	    shotNote = ` Reused shot ${shotId}.`;
415	  }
416	
417	  const asNew = args.as_new === true;
418	  const shouldDefaultPrimaryVariant = taskType === "image-to-image" || taskType === "magic-edit";
419	  const makePrimary = asNew
420	    ? undefined
421	    : typeof args.make_primary === "boolean"
422	      ? args.make_primary
423	      : shouldDefaultPrimaryVariant
424	        ? true
425	        : undefined;
426	  const basedOn = asNew
427	    ? undefined
428	    : (asTrimmedString(args.based_on) ?? (taskType === "video-enhance"
429	      ? selectedVideoEntry?.resolvedContext?.generation_id
430	      : selectedReferenceEntries[0]?.resolvedContext?.generation_id));
431	  const sourceVariantId = TASK_TYPES_SUPPORTING_SOURCE_PLACEMENT.has(taskType)
432	    ? (asTrimmedString(args.source_variant_id) ?? (taskType === "video-enhance"
433	      ? selectedVideoEntry?.resolvedContext?.variant_id
434	      : selectedReferenceEntries[0]?.resolvedContext?.variant_id))
435	    : asTrimmedString(args.source_variant_id);
436	  const generationId = taskType === "image-upscale"
437	    ? selectedReferenceEntries[0]?.resolvedContext?.generation_id
438	    : undefined;
439	  const defaultModelName = taskType === "image-to-video"
440	    ? travelContext?.selectedModel
441	    : (taskType === "text-to-image" || Boolean(TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE[taskType]))
442	      ? imageContext?.defaultModelName
443	      : undefined;
444	  const activeReferenceParams = shouldUseActiveReference && activeReference
445	    ? {
446	      ...(activeReference.styleReferenceStrength !== undefined
447	        ? { style_reference_strength: activeReference.styleReferenceStrength }
448	        : {}),
449	      ...(activeReference.subjectStrength !== undefined
450	        ? { subject_strength: activeReference.subjectStrength }
451	        : {}),
452	      ...(typeof activeReference.subjectDescription === "string" && activeReference.subjectDescription.trim()
453	        ? { subject_description: activeReference.subjectDescription }
454	        : {}),
455	      ...(activeReference.inThisScene !== undefined
456	        ? { in_this_scene: activeReference.inThisScene }
457	        : {}),
458	      ...(activeReference.inThisSceneStrength !== undefined
459	        ? { in_this_scene_strength: activeReference.inThisSceneStrength }
460	        : {}),
461	    }
462	    : undefined;
463	  const effectiveModel = referenceMode
464	    ? "qwen-image"
465	    : (asTrimmedString(args.model) ?? defaultModelName ?? undefined);
466	  const loraCategory = TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE[taskType]
467	    ? "qwen"
468	    : (effectiveModel?.startsWith("z-") ? "z-image" : "qwen");
469	  const loras = imageContext?.selectedLorasByCategory?.[loraCategory] ?? [];
470	  const rawMotion = asFiniteNumber(args.amount_of_motion);
471	  const travelParams = taskType === "image-to-video"
472	    ? {
473	      amount_of_motion: rawMotion !== null
474	        ? rawMotion / 100
475	        : (travelContext ? travelContext.amountOfMotion / 100 : undefined),
476	      steps: asPositiveNumber(args.steps) ?? travelContext?.steps,
477	      guidance_scale: asFiniteNumber(args.guidance_scale) ?? travelContext?.guidanceScale,
478	      enhance_prompt: typeof args.enhance_prompt === "boolean" ? args.enhance_prompt : travelContext?.enhancePrompt,
479	      turbo_mode: typeof args.turbo_mode === "boolean" ? args.turbo_mode : travelContext?.turboMode,
480	      loras: travelContext?.loras?.map((lora) => ({ path: lora.path, strength: lora.strength })),
481	      negative_prompts: travelContext?.negativePrompt ? [travelContext.negativePrompt] : undefined,
482	      text_before_prompts: travelContext?.textBeforePrompts || undefined,
483	      text_after_prompts: travelContext?.textAfterPrompts || undefined,
484	      segment_frames: travelContext ? [travelContext.frames] : undefined,
485	      model_type: travelContext?.generationTypeMode,
486	      generation_mode: travelContext ? (travelContext.generationMode ?? "timeline") : undefined,
487	      phase_config: travelContext?.phaseConfig,
488	    }
489	    : undefined;
490	  // For transfer tasks: start with mode defaults, then overlay active reference params (which may override)
491	  const modeDefaults = referenceMode ? (REFERENCE_MODE_DEFAULTS[referenceMode] ?? {}) : {};
492	  const mergedParams = {
493	    ...modeDefaults,
494	    ...(activeReferenceParams ?? {}),
495	    ...(loras.length > 0 ? { loras } : {}),
496	  };
497	  const filteredTravelParams = travelParams
498	    ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(travelParams).filter(([, value]) => value !== undefined))
499	    : undefined;
500	
501	  const requestedCount = asPositiveNumber(args.count) ?? 1;
502	  const taskParams = taskType === "image-to-video"
503	    ? (filteredTravelParams && Object.keys(filteredTravelParams).length > 0 ? filteredTravelParams : undefined)
504	    : (() => {
505	      const variantParams = {
506	        ...mergedParams,
507	        ...(makePrimary !== undefined ? { is_primary: makePrimary } : {}),
508	      };
509	      return Object.keys(variantParams).length > 0 ? variantParams : undefined;
510	    })();
511	  const taskParamsWithPlacement = withPlacementIntent(taskParams, placementIntent);
512	
513	  // When count > 1 and there's a prompt, expand into varied prompts and create separate tasks
514	  batchTimestamp = null; // Reset so each batch gets a unique timestamp
515	  if (requestedCount > 1 && prompt) {
516	    const targetCount = Math.min(requestedCount, MAX_BATCH_VARIATIONS);
517	    const expandedPrompts = ensureDistinctPrompts(
518	      prompt,
519	      await expandPrompts(prompt, targetCount, logger),
520	      targetCount,
521	    );
522	    let queuedCount = 0;
523	    let failedCount = 0;
524	    let deduplicatedCount = 0;
525	
526	    for (const [index, expandedPrompt] of expandedPrompts.entries()) {
527	      const generationArgs: CreateGenerationTaskArgs = {
528	        project_id: timelineState.projectId,
529	        prompt: expandedPrompt,
530	        count: 1,
531	        task_type: taskType,
532	        reference_mode: referenceMode,
533	        reference_image_url: referenceImageUrls[0],
534	        image_urls: taskType === "image-to-video" ? referenceImageUrls : undefined,
535	        video_url: videoUrl ?? undefined,
536	        strength,
537	        model_name: effectiveModel,
538	        params: taskParamsWithPlacement,
539	        based_on: basedOn ?? undefined,
540	        source_variant_id: sourceVariantId ?? undefined,
541	        generation_id: generationId ?? undefined,
542	        shot_id: shotId ?? undefined,
543	        ...(timelinePlacement ? { timeline_placement: timelinePlacement } : {}),
544	      };
545	
546	      const result = await createGenerationTask({
547	        ...generationArgs,
548	        idempotency_key: await buildBatchIdempotencyKey(generationArgs, index),
549	      });
550	
551	      if (result.result.startsWith("Failed to create task:")) {
552	        failedCount += 1;
553	      } else if (result.result.includes("(deduplicated)")) {
554	        deduplicatedCount += 1;
555	      } else {
556	        queuedCount += 1;
557	      }
558	    }
559	
560	    const summaryParts = [`Queued ${queuedCount} ${queuedCount === 1 ? "task" : "tasks"} with varied prompts.`];
561	    if (failedCount > 0) {
562	      summaryParts.push(`${failedCount} failed.`);
563	    }
564	    if (deduplicatedCount > 0) {
565	      summaryParts.push(`${deduplicatedCount} were duplicates of existing tasks.`);
566	    }
567	    if (requestedCount > MAX_BATCH_VARIATIONS) {
568	      summaryParts.push(`Requested ${requestedCount}, capped at ${MAX_BATCH_VARIATIONS}.`);
569	    }
570	
571	    return { result: `${summaryParts.join(" ")}${shotNote}`.trim() };
572	  }
573	
574	  const result = await createGenerationTask({
575	    project_id: timelineState.projectId,
576	    prompt: prompt ?? undefined,
577	    count: 1,
578	    task_type: taskType,
579	    reference_mode: referenceMode,
580	    reference_image_url: referenceImageUrls[0],
581	    image_urls: taskType === "image-to-video" ? referenceImageUrls : undefined,
582	    video_url: videoUrl ?? undefined,
583	    strength,
584	    model_name: effectiveModel,
585	    params: taskParamsWithPlacement,
586	    based_on: basedOn ?? undefined,
587	    source_variant_id: sourceVariantId ?? undefined,
588	    generation_id: generationId ?? undefined,
589	    shot_id: shotId ?? undefined,
590	    ...(timelinePlacement ? { timeline_placement: timelinePlacement } : {}),
591	  });
592	  return { result: `${result.result}${shotNote}`.trim() };
593	}
594
```
