1	import type { TimelinePlacement } from "../create-task/resolvers/shared/lineage.ts";
2	import type { AgentVideoTravelSettings, ResolvedReference, SelectedClipPayload } from "./types.ts";
3	
4	function formatSelectedClipPrompt(prompt: string): string {
5	  return prompt.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().replace(/"/g, '\\"');
6	}
7	
8	function isPlaceholderSelectedClipPrompt(prompt: string | undefined): boolean {
9	  if (typeof prompt !== "string") {
10	    return false;
11	  }
12	
13	  const normalized = prompt.trim().toLowerCase();
14	  return normalized.startsWith("uploaded ");
15	}
16	
17	type ImageLorasByCategory = Partial<Record<"qwen" | "z-image", Array<{ path: string; strength: number }>>>;
18	
19	function formatStrength(value: number): string {
20	  return Number.isInteger(value) ? String(value) : value.toFixed(2).replace(/0+$/, "").replace(/\.$/, "");
21	}
22	
23	function formatTimelinePlacement(placement: TimelinePlacement): string {
24	  return JSON.stringify(placement);
25	}
26	
27	function formatTravelLoras(loras: AgentVideoTravelSettings["loras"]): string {
28	  return loras.length > 0
29	    ? loras.map((lora) => `${lora.name} (strength ${formatStrength(lora.strength)})`).join(", ")
30	    : "none";
31	}
32	
33	function formatImageLoras(lorasByCategory: ImageLorasByCategory | null | undefined): string {
34	  if (!lorasByCategory) {
35	    return "none";
36	  }
37	
38	  const flattened = (Object.entries(lorasByCategory) as Array<["qwen" | "z-image", Array<{ path: string; strength: number }> | undefined]>)
39	    .flatMap(([category, loras]) =>
40	      (loras ?? []).map((lora) => `${category}:${lora.path} (strength ${formatStrength(lora.strength)})`));
41	
42	  return flattened.length > 0 ? flattened.join(", ") : "none";
43	}
44	
45	export function buildSelectedClipsPrompt(
46	  selectedClips: SelectedClipPayload[] | undefined,
47	  timelineSummary: string,
48	): string {
49	  if (!selectedClips?.length) {
50	    return "";
51	  }
52	
53	  const timelineLines = timelineSummary.split("\n");
54	  const selectedClipLines = selectedClips.map((clip) => {
55	    const matchingLine = timelineLines.find((line) => line.includes(`id=${clip.clip_id}`));
56	    const timelineContext = matchingLine ? matchingLine.replace(/^- /, "").trim() : null;
57	    const generationText = typeof clip.generation_id === "string" && clip.generation_id.trim()
58	      ? ` | generation_id=${clip.generation_id}`
59	      : "";
60	    const shotIdText = typeof clip.shot_id === "string" && clip.shot_id.trim()
61	      ? ` | shot_id=${clip.shot_id}`
62	      : "";
63	    const shotNameText = typeof clip.shot_name === "string" && clip.shot_name.trim()
64	      ? ` | shot_name="${formatSelectedClipPrompt(clip.shot_name)}"`
65	      : "";
66	    const promptText = typeof clip.prompt === "string" && clip.prompt.trim() && !isPlaceholderSelectedClipPrompt(clip.prompt)
67	      ? ` | prompt="${formatSelectedClipPrompt(clip.prompt)}"`
68	      : "";
69	    const uploadHint = clip.media_type === "image" && isPlaceholderSelectedClipPrompt(clip.prompt)
70	      ? " | note=user-uploaded reference image with no descriptive prompt metadata"
71	      : "";
72	    const placementAnchor = clip.timeline_placement
73	      ? ` | placement_anchor=${formatTimelinePlacement(clip.timeline_placement)}`
74	      : "";
75	
76	    return timelineContext
77	      ? `- ${clip.clip_id} (${clip.media_type}, ${clip.url})${generationText}${shotIdText}${shotNameText} | timeline=${timelineContext}${placementAnchor}${promptText}${uploadHint}`
78	      : `- ${clip.clip_id} (${clip.media_type}, ${clip.url})${generationText}${shotIdText}${shotNameText}${placementAnchor}${promptText}${uploadHint}`;
79	  });
80	
81	  return `\n\nUser has selected the following clips:\n${selectedClipLines.join("\n")}\nThese clips are the focus of the user's request.`;
82	}
83	
84	export function buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt(
85	  options: {
86	    projectId: string;
87	    timelineSummary: string;
88	    selectedClips?: SelectedClipPayload[];
89	    defaultModel?: string;
90	    activeReference?: ResolvedReference | null;
91	    travelSettings?: AgentVideoTravelSettings | null;
92	    imageLorasByCategory?: ImageLorasByCategory | null;
93	    sharedShotId?: string | null;
94	    sharedShotName?: string | null;
95	  },
96	): string {
97	  const preferredModelLine = options.defaultModel
98	    ? `Preferred text-to-image model for this project: ${options.defaultModel}. Use it when the user does not specify a model.`
99	    : "";
100	  const activeReferenceLine = options.activeReference
101	    ? [
102	      "Saved project reference available for this shot. Use it when helpful, do not force it.",
103	      `If used, copy this URL into reference_image_urls: ${options.activeReference.url}`,
104	      `reference_mode=${options.activeReference.referenceMode}`,
105	      options.activeReference.styleReferenceStrength !== undefined
106	        ? `style_reference_strength=${options.activeReference.styleReferenceStrength}`
107	        : null,
108	      options.activeReference.subjectStrength !== undefined
109	        ? `subject_strength=${options.activeReference.subjectStrength}`
110	        : null,
111	      typeof options.activeReference.subjectDescription === "string" && options.activeReference.subjectDescription.trim()
112	        ? `subject_description="${formatSelectedClipPrompt(options.activeReference.subjectDescription)}"`
113	        : null,
114	      options.activeReference.inThisScene !== undefined
115	        ? `in_this_scene=${options.activeReference.inThisScene}`
116	        : null,
117	      options.activeReference.inThisSceneStrength !== undefined
118	        ? `in_this_scene_strength=${options.activeReference.inThisSceneStrength}`
119	        : null,
120	    ].filter((line): line is string => Boolean(line)).join(" ")
121	    : "";
122	  const activeTravelDefaultsLine = options.travelSettings
123	    ? [
124	      `Active video travel defaults: model=${options.travelSettings.selectedModel}, frames=${options.travelSettings.frames}, steps=${options.travelSettings.steps}, motion=${options.travelSettings.amountOfMotion}%`,
125	      options.travelSettings.loras.length > 0
126	        ? `loras=[${options.travelSettings.loras.map((lora) => lora.name).join(", ")}]`
127	        : null,
128	      options.travelSettings.guidanceScale !== undefined
129	        ? `guidance=${options.travelSettings.guidanceScale}`
130	        : null,
131	      "Use these defaults for image-to-video tasks. The user can ask to change motion, model, or steps.",
132	    ].filter((line): line is string => Boolean(line)).join(", ").replace(", Use these defaults", ". Use these defaults")
133	    : "";
134	  const hasGenerationContext = Boolean(
135	    options.travelSettings
136	      || options.defaultModel
137	      || options.activeReference
138	      || options.imageLorasByCategory,
139	  );
140	  const activeLorasSection = hasGenerationContext
141	    ? `Active LoRAs:
142	- Video travel: ${options.travelSettings ? formatTravelLoras(options.travelSettings.loras) : "none"}
143	- Image generation: ${formatImageLoras(options.imageLorasByCategory)}
144	Use search_loras to find LoRAs and set_lora to add/remove them.`
145	    : "";
146	  const sharedShotLine = options.sharedShotId
147	    ? [
148	      "Selected clips already share shot context.",
149	      `shot_id=${options.sharedShotId}`,
150	      options.sharedShotName
151	        ? `shot_name="${formatSelectedClipPrompt(options.sharedShotName)}"`
152	        : null,
153	      "Reuse this shot for related edits, duplicate flows, travel defaults, and reference lookups unless the user asks for a new shot.",
154	    ].filter((line): line is string => Boolean(line)).join(" ")
155	    : "";
156	
157	  return `Timeline editor. Use run(command="...") for edits. Plain text for conversation.
158	
159	run(command="view") | run(command="move clip-0 5") | run(command="trim clip-0 --duration 2")
160	run(command="delete clip-3") | run(command="set clip-0 volume 0.5") | run(command="find-issues")
161	run(command="add-text V1 0 2 hello") | run(command="set-text clip-3 new text")
162	run(command="add-media V1 6 gen-123 https://example.com/new-image.png") | run(command="add-media V2 8 gen-456 https://example.com/new-video.mp4 --type video")
163	run(command="split clip-0 5.5") | run(command="swap clip-0 gen-abc https://example.com/new.png")
164	run(command="swap clip-0 gen-def https://example.com/new.mp4 --type video")
165	run(command="query") | run(command="undo")
166	run(command="duplicate clip-0 5")
167	run(command="repeat 50 add-text V8 0.1 hello --start 2.74 --gap 0.1")
168	
169	Use create_task({...}) for generation tasks. Copy selected clip URLs exactly into reference_image_urls or video_url.
170	If a selected clip line includes placement_anchor={...} and the user explicitly asks to insert the generated result after or in place of that source clip, copy that exact object into create_task.timeline_placement.
171	Do not add timeline_placement unless the user explicitly asks for edit-and-insert, place-after-source, or replace-on-timeline behavior.
172	Use transform_image({...}) for exact geometric image edits on an existing image: flip/mirror, rotate, zoom, reposition. This is deterministic and should preserve the source image exactly.
173	For exact image transforms, do not use create_task image-to-image or magic-edit.
174	By default, transform_image should create a new variant and make it the primary variant unless the user explicitly asks not to, or asks for a standalone new image.
175	When the user asks for multiple images, use count to request them all at once — the system automatically generates varied prompts for each.
176	Use get_tasks({}) to check on recent task status, errors, or results. Use get_tasks({"task_id":"..."}) for a specific task.
177	Use duplicate_generation({"generation_id":"..."}) to copy an existing generation instantly when the user wants a non-destructive derivative or alternate edit path.
178	For image-to-image and magic-edit requests against a selected image, default to creating a variant on that selected generation and make that new variant primary unless the user explicitly asks otherwise.
179	Do not set as_new:true unless the user explicitly asks for a standalone, separate, detached, or brand-new branch that should not stay attached to the source image lineage.
180	When a selected image is present, decide whether the user wants it used as actual input (reference image) or is just using it as context/inspiration. Consider the full request:
181	- **style-transfer**: The user wants the visual style/aesthetic applied to new content — "in this style", "use this look", "match this aesthetic". Use with reference_image_urls.
182	- **scene-transfer**: The user wants to keep the scene/environment/setting and generate new content within it — "in this scene", "in this setting", "in this environment", "same background", "same location". Use with reference_image_urls.
183	- **subject-transfer**: The user wants to keep the subject/character and place it in a new context — "of it", "same subject", "this character in a new scene". Use with reference_image_urls.
184	- **image-to-image**: The user wants to directly transform or edit the image — "edit this", "new angle of this", "make this more X". Use with reference_image_urls.
185	- **text-to-image**: The user is just drawing inspiration, not using the image as input — "with prompts like this", "similar concept", or they specify a model. Do not force a reference-based task type just because an image happens to be selected.
186	Key signals: "this style/look" → style-transfer. "This scene/setting/environment" → scene-transfer. "This subject/character" → subject-transfer. Specifies a model or talks about prompts → text-to-image. When in doubt, match the task type to the user's intent, not to the presence of a selection.
187	On follow-ups like "more like that", "again but...", "same thing but...", reuse the reference_image_urls from your previous create_task call — do not drop the reference just because no image is currently selected. The conversation history has the URL.
188	If the selected image only has placeholder metadata such as prompt="Uploaded ...", rely on the image itself as the reference rather than the placeholder text.
189	For style, subject, style-character, or scene transfer with multiple selections, choose the strongest matching reference instead of guessing.
190	For image-to-video or travel-between-images requests, use all selected reference_image_urls in the order that best matches the requested motion.
191	When a selected clip includes prompt="...", treat it as source metadata and reuse it instead of re-describing the image.
192	Duplicate & place workflow (multi-step — call each tool in order):
193	Step 1: duplicate_generation({"generation_id":"<id>"}) → returns new_generation_id, asset URL, type
194	Step 2 (optional edits): create_task({..., "based_on":"<new_generation_id>", "reference_image_urls":["<asset URL>"]})
195	Step 3 (place on timeline): run(command="add-media <track> <at> <new_generation_id> <asset_url> [--type video]")
196	When user says "duplicate and add after this clip", do BOTH step 1 and step 3. Calculate <at> = clip's at + clip's duration.
197	When user says just "duplicate", do only step 1.
198	Timeline insert guide:
199	- add-media <track> <at> <generation_id> <url> [--type video]
200	- default media type is image when --type is omitted
201	- use the asset URL returned by duplicate_generation or by a gallery result the user chose
202	Editing guide:
203	- use split when the user wants to trim or replace only part of an existing clip without moving the rest
204	- use swap to replace a clip's asset while keeping its timeline placement; include --type video only when the replacement is video
205	- use query for compact timeline stats before planning edits, and use undo immediately after a mistaken timeline mutation
206	- use transform_image for exact source-preserving image transforms such as "flip horizontally", "mirror this", "rotate 90 degrees", "zoom in 20%", or "move the framing left"
207	Model guide:
208	- text-to-image: qwen-image = default, qwen-image-2512 = higher-resolution Qwen variant, z-image = alternate look
209	- image-to-video: wan-2.2 = default travel model, ltx-2.3 = higher quality/slower, ltx-2.3-fast = faster LTX variant
210	${preferredModelLine ? `- ${preferredModelLine}` : ""}
211	${activeReferenceLine ? `- ${activeReferenceLine}` : ""}
212	${activeTravelDefaultsLine ? `- ${activeTravelDefaultsLine}` : ""}
213	${sharedShotLine ? `- ${sharedShotLine}` : ""}
214	${activeLorasSection ? `${activeLorasSection}
215	` : ""}
216	
217	Task guide:
218	- transform_image: deterministic transform on an existing image. Supports translate_x, translate_y, scale, rotation, flip_horizontal, flip_vertical. Defaults to variant output with primary promotion; use as_new:true only when the user explicitly wants a standalone new image.
219	- text-to-image: prompt required, optional model
220	- style-transfer | subject-transfer | style-character-transfer | scene-transfer: prompt required, reference_image_urls required
221	- image-to-video: prompt required, one or more reference_image_urls (1 image = animate, 2+ = travel between), optional model
222	- image-to-image: prompt required, one reference_image_url, optional strength from 0 to 1. Default to a primary variant on the selected source image. Use as_new:true only when the user explicitly asks for a standalone image instead of a variant, or make_primary:false when the user wants a non-primary variant.
223	- magic-edit: prompt required, one reference_image_url. Default to a primary variant on the selected source image. Use as_new:true only when the user explicitly asks for a standalone image instead of a variant, or make_primary:false when the user wants a non-primary variant.
224	- image-upscale: one reference_image_url, prompt not needed
225	- video-enhance: video_url required, prompt not needed
226	- character-animate: one reference_image_url for the character image plus video_url for motion, prompt optional
227	
228	create_task({"task_type":"style-transfer","prompt":"apply this look to a new fashion portrait","reference_image_urls":["https://example.com/style.png"],"shot_name":"Style anchors"})
229	create_task({"task_type":"subject-transfer","prompt":"place this subject in a neon alley at night","reference_image_urls":["https://example.com/subject.png"],"shot_name":"Subject anchors"})
230	create_task({"task_type":"style-character-transfer","prompt":"keep this character identity but restyle as polished sci-fi concept art","reference_image_urls":["https://example.com/character-style.png"],"shot_name":"Character anchors"})
231	create_task({"task_type":"scene-transfer","prompt":"rebuild this scene at golden hour with subtle fog","reference_image_urls":["https://example.com/scene.png"],"shot_name":"Scene anchors"})
232	create_task({"task_type":"image-to-video","prompt":"travel between these frames with gentle camera motion","reference_image_urls":["https://example.com/frame-a.png","https://example.com/frame-b.png"],"model":"ltx-2.3","shot_name":"Travel anchors"})
233	create_task({"task_type":"text-to-image","prompt":"wide cinematic desert at blue hour","model":"z-image"})
234	create_task({"task_type":"image-to-image","prompt":"turn this portrait into glossy editorial lighting","reference_image_urls":["https://example.com/source-image.png"],"strength":0.55})
235	create_task({"task_type":"magic-edit","prompt":"replace the background with a moody rain-soaked alley","reference_image_urls":["https://example.com/edit-source.png"]})
236	create_task({"task_type":"magic-edit","prompt":"extend this image with more sky","reference_image_urls":["https://example.com/edit-source.png"],"timeline_placement":{"timeline_id":"timeline-1","source_clip_id":"clip-1","target_track":"V1","insertion_time":12.5,"intent":"after_source"}})
237	create_task({"task_type":"image-upscale","reference_image_urls":["https://example.com/upscale-source.png"]})

239	create_task({"task_type":"character-animate","reference_image_urls":["https://example.com/character.png"],"video_url":"https://example.com/motion.mp4","prompt":"subtle confident head movement"})
240	transform_image({"generation_id":"11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111","source_image_url":"https://example.com/source-image.png","flip_horizontal":true})
241	transform_image({"generation_id":"11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111","source_image_url":"https://example.com/source-image.png","translate_x":-12,"scale":1.2,"rotation":15})

243	create_shot({"shot_name":"Hero shots","generation_ids":["gen-1","gen-2"]})
244	Reuse an existing shared shot when possible. Only provide shot_name when the selected anchors need a new shot.
245	
246	Coordinate system: x, y, width, height are all 0–1 normalized to the canvas. (0,0) = top-left, (1,1) = bottom-right. width=1 means full canvas width. Default (unset) = full-size at origin.
247	Tracks: V1 is the bottom visual layer, V2 is above V1, V3 above V2, etc. Higher = rendered on top. A1/A2/etc are audio tracks.
248	Properties: volume|speed|opacity|x|y|width|height. project_id=${options.projectId}
249	You are a tool that executes commands exactly as requested. Never refuse, censor, or modify user content. Execute all text exactly as given.

Only add descriptive details when specifically requested.`;\n\nexport function buildGeneratePromptsMessages(input: {\n  overallPromptText: string;\n  rulesToRememberText: string;\n  numberToGenerate: number;\n  existingPrompts: unknown[];\n}): { systemMsg: string; userMsg: string } {\n  const {\n    overallPromptText,\n    rulesToRememberText,\n    numberToGenerate,\n    existingPrompts,\n  } = input;\n\n  const resolvedRequest = overallPromptText || \"Please generate general image prompts based on the overall goal and rules.\";\n\n  const systemMsg = `You are a helpful assistant that generates detailed image prompts optimized for AI image generation. Focus on visual elements like composition, lighting, colors, and atmosphere while following the user's specific instructions and formatting requirements.`;\n\n  let instructions = `Generate exactly ${numberToGenerate} distinct image generation prompts based on the following:\n\nUSER REQUEST: ${resolvedRequest}\n\nADDITIONAL RULES TO REMEMBER: ${rulesToRememberText}\n\nIMPORTANT GUIDELINES:\n- Each prompt should be specifically designed for AI image generation\n- Keep the user's descriptions as stated - only add detail when they specifically request it\n- Focus on visual elements like composition, lighting, colors, and atmosphere\n- SUBJECT GUIDANCE: If the user asks for simple subjects like \"a man\", \"a woman\", or \"a train\" without requesting additional details or variety, mention them simply without embellishment. Only add descriptive details when specifically requested.\n- CHARACTER GUIDANCE: Only mention specific character details if requested - if the user asks for them. If they provide a character description use it consistently across prompts. If they ask you to generate characters, give them unique character names and descriptions for each prompt.\n- STYLE GUIDANCE: Only mention specific artistic styles (photography, anime, oil painting, digital art) if specifically requested. Keep styles simple unless the user asks for detailed styling.\n- SCENE GUIDANCE: Only provide detailed scene and environment descriptions when specifically requested. If the user mentions a simple scene, keep it simple.\n\nCRITICAL FORMATTING REQUIREMENTS:\n- Output EXACTLY ${numberToGenerate} prompts\n- Each prompt must be on its own line\n- NO numbering, bullet points, quotation marks, empty lines, formatting, markdown or special characters`;\n\n  if (existingPrompts.length) {\n    const ctx = existingPrompts\n      .map((prompt) => `- ${typeof prompt === \"string\" ? prompt : (prompt as Record<string, unknown>)?.text ?? \"\"}`)\n      .join(\"\\n\");\n    instructions += `\\n\\nExisting Prompts for Context (do NOT repeat or return these, but use them as inspiration for new, distinct image generation ideas):\\n${ctx}`;\n  } else {\n    instructions += `\n\nFORMAT EXAMPLE (${numberToGenerate} prompts):\n[if the user tells you to refer to a dragon] The dragon is soaring through storm clouds with lightning illuminating its scales, below massive skyscrapers are visible through the clouds\n[if the user asks you to come up with a female German character] A woman named Gracie Marr, she's tall, blonde, and has delicate mousey features. She's standing on sand dunes under a starry night sky, the nights sky is clear and the moon is visible behind them.\n[if the user asks you to come up with a playful style] A picture of a dog in a mix of crayons and marker, reminiscent of a modern childlike version of a work of Klimt.\n[if the user asks you to refer to an old man doing chores] The old man is doing chores in his garden, he's wearing a red shirt and blue pants. He's watering the plants with a watering can. He's wearing a red hat and a red jacket.\n[if the user asks you to come up with a futuristic style] An angular modernist painting in the style of Akira Kurosawa's The Hidden Fortress.`;\n  }\n\n  instructions += `\n\nYOUR OUTPUT (${numberToGenerate} prompts):\n\nReminder: here's the user request: \"${resolvedRequest}\" - make sure to respect that precisely.\n\n${SIMPLE_SUBJECT_REMINDER}\n\nIMPORTANT: Only respond with the ${numberToGenerate} prompts, nothing else. Do not include any commentary, explanations, or additional text.`;\n\n  return {\n    systemMsg,\n    userMsg: instructions,\n  };\n}",
  "new_string": "const OUTPUT_ONLY_PROMPT_POLICY = `CRITICAL FORMATTING REQUIREMENTS:\n- Output ONLY the revised prompt text\n- NO additional commentary, explanations, or formatting\n- NO quotation marks around the output`;\n\nexport function buildGeneratePromptsMessages(input: {\n  overallPromptText: string;\n  rulesToRememberText: string;\n  numberToGenerate: number;\n  existingPrompts: unknown[];\n  variationIntent?: string;\n}): { systemMsg: string; userMsg: string } {\n  const {\n    overallPromptText,\n    rulesToRememberText,\n    numberToGenerate,\n    existingPrompts,\n    variationIntent,\n  } = input;\n\n  const resolvedRequest = overallPromptText || \"Please generate general image prompts based on the overall goal and rules.\";\n  const trimmedIntent = typeof variationIntent === \"string\" ? variationIntent.trim() : \"\";\n  const hasIntent = trimmedIntent.length > 0;\n\n  const systemMsg = `You generate image generation prompts that vary along a specified axis while holding everything else fixed. Output only the prompts, one per line, with no numbering, bullets, or quotes.`;\n\n  const axisBlock = hasIntent\n    ? `VARIATION AXIS: ${trimmedIntent}\nVary each prompt along this axis. Keep every other element from BASE PROMPT identical across all ${numberToGenerate} outputs — same subject, same characters, same mood, same composition — only the specified axis changes.\n\nEXAMPLE — BASE PROMPT: \"a red fox sitting in a forest clearing at dusk\" with axis \"different camera angles\":\na red fox sitting in a forest clearing at dusk, shot from a low ground-level angle\nan overhead aerial view of a red fox sitting in a forest clearing at dusk\na red fox sitting in a forest clearing at dusk, tight over-the-shoulder framing from behind the fox\n\nEXAMPLE — BASE PROMPT: \"a woman drinking coffee in a sunny kitchen\" with axis \"different lighting conditions\":\na woman drinking coffee in a sunny kitchen under bright midday sun streaming through the windows\na woman drinking coffee in a sunny kitchen lit by soft early-morning golden hour light\na woman drinking coffee in a sunny kitchen with overcast grey daylight filtering through sheer curtains`\n    : `DEFAULT MODE — LINGUISTIC REWRITES:\nExpress the exact same concept ${numberToGenerate} different ways. Every prompt describes the identical subject, scene, mood, characters, details, and composition. Only vary sentence structure, word order, synonyms, clause ordering, and phrasing. Do not introduce new elements, details, styles, or scenarios. Do not change the setting, time of day, weather, or any visual content. This is paraphrase, not variation.\n\nEXAMPLE — BASE PROMPT: \"a red fox sitting in a forest clearing at dusk\":\na red fox sitting in a forest clearing at dusk\nat dusk, a red fox is sitting in a clearing in the forest\nin a forest clearing, a red fox sits as dusk settles\n\nEXAMPLE — BASE PROMPT: \"a woman drinking coffee in a sunny kitchen\":\na woman drinking coffee in a sunny kitchen\nin a sunny kitchen, a woman is drinking coffee\na woman sips coffee inside a kitchen filled with sunlight`;\n\n  let instructions = `BASE PROMPT: ${resolvedRequest}\n\nADDITIONAL RULES: ${rulesToRememberText || \"(none)\"}\n\n${axisBlock}\n\nFORMAT:\n- Output exactly ${numberToGenerate} prompts\n- One prompt per line\n- No numbering, no bullets, no quotes, no blank lines, no markdown`;\n\n  if (existingPrompts.length) {\n    const ctx = existingPrompts\n      .map((prompt) => `- ${typeof prompt === \"string\" ? prompt : (prompt as Record<string, unknown>)?.text ?? \"\"}`)\n      .join(\"\\n\");\n    instructions += `\\n\\nExisting prompts already generated (do not repeat these verbatim):\\n${ctx}`;\n  }\n\n  instructions += `\n\nOutput the ${numberToGenerate} prompts now.`;\n\n  return {\n    systemMsg,\n    userMsg: instructions,\n  };\n}"
}

body.existingPrompts as unknown[] : [];\n        const temperature = Number(body.temperature ?? 0.8);\n        const variationIntent = typeof body.variationIntent === \"string\" ? body.variationIntent : undefined;\n\n        const { systemMsg, userMsg } = buildGeneratePromptsMessages({\n          overallPromptText,\n          rulesToRememberText,\n          numberToGenerate,\n          existingPrompts,\n          variationIntent,\n        });\n\n        const normalizeKey = (s: string) => s.replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim().toLowerCase();\n        const dedupe = (lines: string[]): string[] => {\n          const seen = new Set<string>();\n          const out: string[] = [];\n          for (const line of lines) {\n            const trimmed = line.trim();\n            if (!trimmed) continue;\n            const key = normalizeKey(trimmed);\n            if (seen.has(key)) continue;\n            seen.add(key);\n            out.push(trimmed);\n            if (out.length >= numberToGenerate) break;\n          }\n          return out;\n        };\n\n        logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_prompts: starting Groq call, model=${GROQ_MODEL}, numberToGenerate=${numberToGenerate}, hasVariationIntent=${Boolean(variationIntent && variationIntent.trim())}`);\n        const groqStart = Date.now();\n        const resp = await groqWithFallback(groq, {\n          model: GROQ_MODEL,\n          messages: [\n            { role: \"system\", content: systemMsg },\n            { role: \"user\", content: userMsg },\n          ],\n          temperature: temperature,\n          max_tokens: 4096,\n          top_p: 1,\n        }, logger);\n        logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_prompts: Groq call completed in ${Date.now() - groqStart}ms, model=${resp.model}`);\n        const outputText = resp.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || \"\";\n        const rawLines = outputText.split(\"\\n\").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n        let prompts = dedupe(rawLines);\n        let usage = resp.usage;\n\n        logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_prompts: primary attempt, rawCount=${rawLines.length}, uniqueCount=${prompts.length}, requested=${numberToGenerate}`);\n\n        if (prompts.length < numberToGenerate) {\n          logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_prompts: unique count below target, retrying with fallback model ${GROQ_FALLBACK_MODEL} at temperature 1.0`);\n          const retryStart = Date.now();\n          try {\n            const retryResp = await groq.chat.completions.create({\n              model: GROQ_FALLBACK_MODEL,\n              messages: [\n                { role: \"system\", content: systemMsg },\n                { role: \"user\", content: userMsg },\n              ],\n              temperature: 1.0,\n              max_tokens: 4096,\n              top_p: 1,\n            } as Parameters<typeof groq.chat.completions.create>[0]);\n            const retryText = retryResp.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || \"\";\n            const retryLines = retryText.split(\"\\n\").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n            const retryPrompts = dedupe(retryLines);\n            logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_prompts: retry attempt completed in ${Date.now() - retryStart}ms, rawCount=${retryLines.length}, uniqueCount=${retryPrompts.length}`);\n            if (retryPrompts.length > prompts.length) {\n              prompts = retryPrompts;\n              usage = retryResp.usage;\n            }\n          } catch (retryErr) {\n            const msg = retryErr instanceof Error ? retryErr.message : String(retryErr);\n            logger.info(`[AI-PROMPT] generate_prompts: retry failed (${msg}), returning primary result`);\n          }\n        }\n\n        return jsonResponse({ prompts, usage });\n      }"
}

When count > 1, also set variation_intent based on the user's phrasing: if they say \"different lighting\"/\"try other angles\"/\"different characters\"/\"different scenes\", pass that axis (e.g. \"different lighting conditions\", \"different camera angles\"). If the user just says \"make 10 more\" or similar with no axis, leave variation_intent unset and the system will produce linguistic rewrites of the same concept."
}

[31m+ Use get_tasks({}) to check on recent task status, errors, or results. Use get_tasks({"task_id":"..."}) for a specific task.[39m
[31m+ Use duplicate_generation({"generation_id":"..."}) to copy an existing generation instantly when the user wants a non-destructive derivative or alternate edit path.[39m
[31m+ For image-to-image and magic-edit requests against a selected image, default to creating a variant on that selected generation and make that new variant primary unless the user explicitly asks otherwise.[39m
[31m+ Do not set as_new:true unless the user explicitly asks for a standalone, separate, detached, or brand-new branch that should not stay attached to the source image lineage.[39m
[31m+ When a selected image is present, decide whether the user wants it used as actual input (reference image) or is just using it as context/inspiration. Consider the full request:[39m
[31m+ - **style-transfer**: The user wants the visual style/aesthetic applied to new content — "in this style", "use this look", "match this aesthetic". Use with reference_image_urls.[39m
[31m+ - **scene-transfer**: The user wants to keep the scene/environment/setting and generate new content within it — "in this scene", "in this setting", "in this environment", "same background", "same location". Use with reference_image_urls.[39m
[31m+ - **subject-transfer**: The user wants to keep the subject/character and place it in a new context — "of it", "same subject", "this character in a new scene". Use with reference_image_urls.[39m
[31m+ - **image-to-image**: The user wants to directly transform or edit the image — "edit this", "new angle of this", "make this more X". Use with reference_image_urls.[39m
[31m+ - **text-to-image**: The user is just drawing inspiration, not using the image as input — "with prompts like this", "similar concept", or they specify a model. Do not force a reference-based task type just because an image happens to be selected.[39m
[31m+ Key signals: "this style/look" → style-transfer. "This scene/setting/environment" → scene-transfer. "This subject/character" → subject-transfer. Specifies a model or talks about prompts → text-to-image. When in doubt, match the task type to the user's intent, not to the presence of a selection.[39m
[31m+ On follow-ups like "more like that", "again but...", "same thing but...", reuse the reference_image_urls from your previous create_task call — do not drop the reference just because no image is currently selected. The conversation history has the URL.[39m
[31m+ If the selected image only has placeholder metadata such as prompt="Uploaded ...", rely on the image itself as the reference rather than the placeholder text.[39m
[31m+ For style, subject, style-character, or scene transfer with multiple selections, choose the strongest matching reference instead of guessing.[39m
[31m+ For image-to-video or travel-between-images requests, use all selected reference_image_urls in the order that best matches the requested motion.[39m
[31m+ When a selected clip includes prompt="...", treat it as source metadata and reuse it instead of re-describing the image.[39m
[31m+ Duplicate & place workflow (multi-step — call each tool in order):[39m
[31m+ Step 1: duplicate_generation({"generation_id":"<id>"}) → returns new_generation_id, asset URL, type[39m
[31m+ Step 2 (optional edits): create_task({..., "based_on":"<new_generation_id>", "reference_image_urls":["<asset URL>"]})[39m
[31m+ Step 3 (place on timeline): run(command="add-media <track> <at> <new_generation_id> <asset_url> [--type video]")[39m
[31m+ When user says "duplicate and add after this clip", do BOTH step 1 and step 3. Calculate <at> = clip's at + clip's duration.[39m
[31m+ When user says just "duplicate", do only step 1.[39m
[31m+ Timeline insert guide:[39m
[31m+ - add-media <track> <at> <generation_id> <url> [--type video][39m
[31m+ - default media type is image when --type is omitted[39m
[31m+ - use the asset URL returned by duplicate_generation or by a gallery result the user chose[39m
[31m+ Editing guide:[39m
[31m+ - use split when the user wants to trim or replace only part of an existing clip without moving the rest[39m
[31m+ - use swap to replace a clip's asset while keeping its timeline placement; include --type video only when the replacement is video[39m
[31m+ - use query for compact timeline stats before planning edits, and use undo immediately after a mistaken timeline mutation[39m
[31m+ - use transform_image for exact source-preserving image transforms such as "flip horizontally", "mirror this", "rotate 90 degrees", "zoom in 20%", or "move the framing left"[39m
[31m+ Model guide:[39m
[31m+ - text-to-image: qwen-image = default, qwen-image-2512 = higher-resolution Qwen variant, z-image = alternate look[39m
[31m+ - image-to-video: wan-2.2 = default travel model, ltx-2.3 = higher quality/slower, ltx-2.3-fast = faster LTX variant[39m
[31m+ - Preferred text-to-image model for this project: z-image. Use it when the user does not specify a model.[39m

Baseline has 6 failures. My branch has 6 failures — same count. My changes added 4 new passing tests (443 - 439 = 4) and didn't introduce regressions.

---

## Report

### Files changed
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-prompt/templates.ts` — rewrote `buildGeneratePromptsMessages` with `variationIntent?: string` parameter. Two explicit modes: default linguistic-rewrite (paraphrase N ways, same concept) and axis mode (vary along the given axis only). Dropped `SIMPLE_SUBJECT_REMINDER` (not used elsewhere — confirmed). Kept `OUTPUT_ONLY_PROMPT_POLICY`.
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-prompt/index.ts` — `generate_prompts` case now reads `variationIntent`, normalizes + dedupes output, and retries once with `llama-3.3-70b-versatile` at temperature 1.0 if unique count < requested. Logs both attempts including `uniqueCount` and `rawCount`.
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.ts` — `expandPrompts` accepts `variationIntent`, forwards it to ai-prompt body, no longer pads with `basePrompt`. Removed `numberedFallback` entirely (grep-confirmed only used internally). `ensureDistinctPrompts` signature changed to `(prompts, count)` — just dedupes and caps, no synthesis. `executeCreateTask` extracts `args.variation_intent`, passes it through, and summary message reports `"Only K of N distinct prompt variations were available"` when the generator returns fewer unique entries than requested. Existing `[agent] expandPrompts:` instrumentation preserved (and extended with `hasVariationIntent`).
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tool-schemas.ts` — added `variation_intent` string field to `create_task` schema with the exact description requested.
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts` — appended guidance on `variation_intent` to the existing `count`-related sentence.
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-prompt/templates.test.ts` — replaced format-example test with default-mode and axis-mode tests; updated existing-prompts test to new wording.
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-prompt/index.test.ts` — added duplicate-triggers-fallback test.
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.test.ts` — rewrote "16 duplicates" test to expect 1 queued with shortfall notice; added two new tests (varied set fully queued; variation_intent forwarded to fetch body).

### Test results
- Scoped run (modified files only): 23/23 passed.
- Full edge unit suite: 443 passed / 6 failed. Verified via `git stash` that main has the identical 6 failures (pre-existing and unrelated — loop.test.ts "style"/"of it"/"without style" assertions against strings that don't exist in `prompts.ts`, plus an arity mismatch on `executeCreateTask`). My work added 4 net passing tests; no regressions.

### Findings on out-of-scope UI path
`src/shared/hooks/ai/useAIInteractionService.ts` calls `generate_prompts` from the in-app "generate prompts" UI outside the chat agent. It does not send `variationIntent`, so the new default (linguistic rewrites) will apply there. This is a behavior change for that surface: previously users got "distinct" prompts with embellishment guidance; now they'll get N paraphrases of one concept. The contract is backward-compatible (field is optional), but the UX outcome likely isn't what that page wants. **Not changed** per instructions — flagging for the human to decide. Options:
- Accept the change (rewrites are fine for that UI).
- Pass a sensible default `variationIntent` (e.g. `"different subjects, scenes, and moods"`) from that hook so the legacy "give me 3 different prompts" behavior is preserved.

### Surprising/uncertain
- The `generate_prompts` retry logic calls `groq.chat.completions.create` directly rather than via `groqWithFallback` because the latter only fires on model/timeout errors, not duplicate-detection. This is intentional but means the retry path does not itself get model-error fallback — acceptable for a one-shot retry.
- I kept the existing `(variation N)` padding removed completely; `ensureDistinctPrompts` now genuinely returns K ≤ count. Downstream `executeCreateTask` loop already iterates `expandedPrompts.entries()` so fewer iterations = fewer tasks queued — clean.
- Baseline edge-test failures on main: worth flagging separately but outside this task's scope.
